## Sprint 21 (2026-06-06) — shared identification (cédula) input

No migration needed (storage format unchanged: clean digits).

- New shared field **`CedulaInput`** (`src/components/ui/cedula-input.tsx`) + pure helpers (`src/lib/cedula.ts`: `cleanId`, `formatId`, `detectIdType`, `idTypeLabel`, `normalizeId`, `isValidId`). Reused in **professional registration** (email/pw + OAuth steps), the **client quick-register modal**, the **booking "completar perfil" step**, and **/completar-perfil**.
- **Live masking:** national cédula formats progressively as `X-XXXX-XXXX` (province · 4-digit tomo · 4-digit asiento); from 10 digits on (NITE/DIMEX) the digits show seamlessly. Digits-only — non-numerics are stripped. `inputMode="numeric"` + `pattern` for the mobile numeric keypad.
- **Storage:** always the **clean value (digits only, no hyphens)** e.g. `101230456` — required by TSE/Hacienda/SINPE. The mask produces fixed-width segments, so the clean digits are already the normalised (zero-padded) form; hyphens are display-only.
- **Auto-detection** by length/first digit (no manual selector): 9 digits + first 1–9 → cédula nacional; 10 → NITE; 11–12 → DIMEX. Detected type shows as a subtle badge in the label once enough digits are entered.
- **Validation = format/length only.** No local check digit — the CR cédula has **no published control digit**; real validation is a live TSE/Registro Civil padrón lookup (not wired here). Locally we only check length + allowed first digit (`isValidId`). Registration keeps its existing zod `validateCedulaFormat` (same rules) and real-time duplicate check.
- **Label** renamed "Número de cédula" → **"Número de identificación"** (covers cédula, DIMEX, NITE). The `required` prop drives the suffix: `true` → red `*`; `false` → "(opcional)".
- **Required-state choices:** professional registration = **required** (mandatory). Client side (quick-register modal, booking completar-perfil, /completar-perfil) is also kept **required** — those flows already collected and depend on the ID to confirm the account/booking, so loosening it would break downstream logic; the `required` prop is in place to switch any client usage to "(opcional)" later with no code change.

## Sprint 20 (2026-06-06) — unified workplaces, phone selector fix, location flow order

**Run this migration in Supabase SQL Editor:**
```
022_professional_workplaces.sql  -- professionals.workplaces jsonb [{id,name,address,lat,lng}] (supersedes affiliations)
```

- **Unified workplaces:** the separate "Instituciones o lugares donde trabajás" text list AND the single fixed-location map pin are merged into one **`workplaces jsonb`** list. New `WorkplacesPicker` (Google Places autocomplete + map): searching/selecting a place — or clicking the map — **adds** a workplace `{id,name,address,lat,lng}`; multiple allowed; each is removable. Used in registration (when fixed-location is chosen) and the profile editor. Each workplace is a **pin on /buscar** (map data flat-maps one pin per workplace) and a **workplace entry on the profile** ("Lugares de trabajo"). Searchable via `workplaces::text.ilike`. `lat/lng` synced to `workplaces[0]`.
- **PhoneInput fixed (task B):** flags now render as **images** (flagcdn) instead of emoji (which don't show in `<select>` on Windows); selecting a country now **sticks** (internal `code` state instead of deriving solely from the value — picking a country with an empty number no longer snaps back to CR); number input is empty by default (no placeholder digits); the country drives the **exact** national digit length (input capped + live "N/len" hint) and registration **blocks submit** unless `isPhoneComplete(value)` (national length === country length). Stored value is the full number with country code.
- **Registration location flow reordered (task C):** **province → cantón** (both **required**, manual dropdowns, no auto-fill) come **first**, then "¿Cómo ofrecés tus servicios?" (travel / fixed), then — only for fixed — the **optional** workplaces map for precise pins. Province/cantón drive search filtering; the pin is only for showing precise location. Removed the old pin→province/canton auto-fill (`matchProvinceCanton` no longer wired in; `LocationPicker` retired in favor of `WorkplacesPicker`).

## Sprint 19 (2026-06-06) — flexible professional identity

**Run this migration in Supabase SQL Editor:**
```
021_professional_affiliations.sql  -- professionals.affiliations text[] (business_name already from 019; account_type now unused)
```

- Replaced the binary **persona física / empresa** choice with a **flexible identity model**. Beyond the required personal name (`profiles.full_name`), everything is optional and editable later from profile settings:
  - `business_name` — a brand/business name they operate under.
  - `affiliations text[]` — one or more institutions/workplaces they're affiliated with (chips: type + Enter / "Agregar").
- **Registration** (`/registro/profesional`): account-type toggle removed; the service step now has an optional "Identidad" card (brand name + affiliations). Personal name still required (step-0 for email/pw, OAuth name field otherwise).
- **Profile editor**: account-type toggle removed; name field is always the personal name; added optional brand name + affiliations. Save retries without the optional columns if the DB isn't migrated yet.
- **Register API**: accepts `businessName` + `affiliations` (dropped `accountType`); `full_name` is always the personal name; graceful retry covers `affiliations` too.

## Stale-session crash fix (normal browser showed "Algo salió mal", incognito worked)

ROOT CAUSE: a saved-but-stale/invalid Supabase refresh token. `supabase.auth.getUser()` called unguarded during SSR (e.g. the public `/buscar` page, `getAdminUser`, middleware) would THROW an `AuthApiError` during the silent refresh instead of returning a null user; in a Server Component that thrown error bubbled to the `[locale]/error.tsx` boundary → generic error screen. Incognito has no token, so the refresh never happened.

GRACEFUL FIX:
- `src/lib/supabase/get-user.ts` → `safeGetUser(supabase)`: getUser that never throws (returns null on any auth error). Used in `/buscar` page SSR and `getAdminUser` (so `getApiAdmin`/`requireAdmin` are covered too).
- Middleware: now validates/refreshes the session for any request carrying an `sb-*-auth-token` cookie (incognito/anon skip it — fast path). getUser wrapped in try/catch; on a stale/invalid token it CLEARS the auth cookies (`maxAge:0`) so the browser becomes cleanly logged out and stops re-triggering on every visit, and redirects keep those cookie changes. Anonymous users on protected routes still redirect to login.
- `useAuth` (client): `getSession()` wrapped with `.catch` → on a corrupt/stale local session it `signOut()`s and treats the user as logged out instead of surfacing a broken UI.
- The generic error boundary stays for truly unexpected errors only.

## Security audit & hardening (migration 047 + app layer)

Findings and fixes from the security pass. **SQL was run as migration 047** (must be applied in Supabase).

### 1. RLS / sensitive data — CRITICAL fix
- **FOUND:** `profiles` had a `USING (true)` SELECT policy → the *public anon key* could read EVERY column of EVERY profile, including **cédula, email, phone** and moderation fields. Anyone could dump all cédulas.
- **FIXED (migration 047):** column-level privileges — `REVOKE SELECT ON profiles FROM anon, authenticated` then `GRANT SELECT (id, full_name, avatar_url, role, is_disabled, created_at, updated_at)`. Public professional cards + reviewer names (which embed `profiles(full_name, avatar_url)`) still work; cédula/email/phone are now readable only by the **owner** (via the new `get_my_profile()` SECURITY DEFINER RPC, returns only `auth.uid()`'s row) and the **service-role** (admin). Owner code migrated to `rpc("get_my_profile")` / safe-column embeds (pro dashboard, cliente dashboard, completar-perfil, registro pro, booking modal).
- **Verified OK:** support_tickets/support_ticket_messages/reports/notification_deliveries/padron are RLS-locked to the service-role; `/api/support` scopes every read to `user_id`; notifications/projects/proposals/saved_professionals/reviews/bookings are owner-scoped; the proposals route only returns a client's phone for an **accepted** proposal (service-role gated). Reference tables (categories/provincias/cantones) are intentionally public.
- **KNOWN FOLLOW-UP (documented, not fixed to avoid breaking the public listing):** `professionals` is `USING(true)` public-read and still exposes internal moderation columns (`banned_reason`, `verification_reason`, `id_document_note`). The public queries don't select them, but a crafted query could. Safe remediation = move these to a service-role-only `professional_moderation` table (the owner dashboard does `select("*")`, so a column-grant would need the owner select rewritten first).

### 2. Auth & access control — OK
- Admin is enforced server-side: every `/api/admin/*` route calls `getApiAdmin()` and admin pages call `requireAdmin()` (role confirmed via the service-role against `profiles.role`, never trusting the client). Protected `/dashboard/*` gated in middleware + page-level auth checks.

### 3. Input validation / injection
- No `dangerouslySetInnerHTML` anywhere — React auto-escapes UGC (names, bios, messages, reviews). Queries go through supabase-js/PostgREST (parameterized; no raw SQL concatenation).
- **FIXED:** `searchProfessionals` interpolated the raw search `q` into a PostgREST `.or()` filter → filter-injection / LIKE-wildcard abuse. Now sanitized (strip `, ( ) : * % _ \ " '`, cap 80 chars) before interpolation.

### 4. Sensitive data exposure
- **FIXED:** `/api/cedula/[id]` (returns a person's official padrón name) was unauthenticated → an open name-by-cédula enumeration API. Hardened with a per-IP **rate limit**. ⚠️ **NOTE (reversed 2026-06-12):** the original hardening here *also* added an authenticated-session gate, but that broke pre-auth professional registration (cédula lookup runs before the account exists) — see "URGENT FIX #2" above. The session gate was removed; anti-abuse for this endpoint is the rate limit ONLY. The padrón table itself stays private via the `padron_lookup` SECURITY DEFINER RPC. Do not re-add an auth gate.
- Admin user profile API masks cédula (`maskId`); full cédula only in the verification case file (admin/service-role). No secrets in the repo (`.env*` gitignored; only the public anon key is client-side; service-role key is server-only via `createAdminClient`).

### 5. Rate limiting
- Added an in-memory per-IP limiter (`src/lib/rate-limit.ts`) on abuse-prone endpoints: `/api/cedula` (20/min), `/api/contact` (5/min), `/api/support` reply (10/min), `/api/report-*` (10/min). NOTE: per-instance memory — best-effort on serverless; a shared store (Upstash/Redis) is the follow-up for global limits.

### 6. General hardening
- **Security headers** added in `next.config.ts` for all routes: HSTS (2y, preload), `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`, `X-Frame-Options: SAMEORIGIN` + CSP `frame-ancestors 'self'` (anti-clickjacking), `Referrer-Policy: strict-origin-when-cross-origin`, `Permissions-Policy` (camera/mic/payment/usb off, geolocation self).
- Auth cookies are managed by `@supabase/ssr` (httpOnly/secure/sameSite). HTTPS enforced by host + HSTS.
- **Dependencies:** bumped `nodemailer` 6→8 (fixes the high advisory). Remaining: 2 moderate, build-time only (postcss CSS-stringify XSS; a next advisory whose "fix" is an impossible downgrade) — low real-world risk, tracked.

### Manual actions required
- **Run migration `047_profiles_column_security.sql`** in Supabase (plus the still-pending `043`–`046`).
- Consider a shared-store rate limiter (Upstash) and the `professional_moderation` table split for defense-in-depth.

---

## RLS re-audit (focused: padron / profiles / professionals / admin / write policies) — migration 048

Second focused pass confirming the items below; new fixes in migration **048** (run it in Supabase).

1. **PADRON (cédulas+names) — SAFE.** `padron`/`padron_staging` have RLS ON with NO policies (deny-all to anon/authenticated). The ONLY code touching them is `src/lib/verification/identity-verifier.ts` and `/api/admin/providers/[id]` — both via `createAdminClient()` (service-role, server-only). The admin UI (`admin-case.tsx`) only renders the comparison object returned by the admin-guarded API; it never queries padron. No client-side padron access exists.

2. **PROFILES — fixed in 047 (must be applied).** Sensitive columns (`cedula`, `email`, `phone`, moderation flags) are no longer readable by anon/authenticated — column-level grants expose only `id, full_name, avatar_url, role, is_disabled, created_at, updated_at`; the owner reads their own sensitive fields via `get_my_profile()`. **PROFESSIONALS (new, 048):** internal moderation columns `banned_reason`/`verification_reason`/`id_document_note` are now revoked from the anon role (catalog-computed grant of all other columns); public cards/profiles are unaffected (they never select those). Public contact fields (`whatsapp`, `call_phone`) remain intentionally public. Residual: a logged-in (authenticated) user could still read those 3 via a crafted query (no UI exposes them) — full lockdown needs the owner dashboard's `select('*')` rewritten or a separate `professional_moderation` table (documented follow-up).

3. **ADMIN tables — SAFE.** `reports`, `support_tickets`, `support_ticket_messages`, `notification_deliveries` have RLS ON with no user policy → service-role only. Admin reads them through admin-guarded API routes (`getApiAdmin`). Users read their OWN tickets only via `/api/support` (every query scoped to `user_id`); they cannot read others' tickets/messages/reports.

4. **Over-permissive write policy — FIXED (048).** Legacy `support_messages` (migration 012, unused) had `"Anyone can insert support messages" WITH CHECK (true)` = world-writable (anon spam/storage abuse). Dropped it (RLS stays on → service-role only; owner-scoped SELECT kept). All other write policies are owner-scoped (`auth.uid() = client_id/id/profile_id/suggested_by`). Public-read tables (categories/provincias/cantones/insurers/blocked_dates/availability_slots/reviews) expose only non-sensitive data.

5. **Service-role key — SAFE.** `createAdminClient()` uses `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY` (NOT `NEXT_PUBLIC_*`); no client component imports it; only `NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL` + `NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY` reach the browser (anon key is public by design).

**Manual action:** apply migrations **047 and 048** in Supabase (plus pending `043`–`046`). Until 047 runs, the profiles cédula exposure is still live.

## Realtime subscription ordering fix (app-wide crash)

SYMPTOM: "Error: cannot add `postgres_changes` callbacks for realtime:notifications after `subscribe()`" → error boundary "Algo salió mal", breaking login/registration/page loads.

ROOT CAUSE: `NotificationBell` (`src/components/notifications/notification-bell.tsx`) used a STATIC channel topic `supabase.channel("notifications")`. The navbar mounts the bell in BOTH header rows (default + compact), and dashboards mount one too — so multiple instances requested the same topic. Supabase returns the CACHED channel by topic name, so the 2nd instance's `.on('postgres_changes', …)` ran on an already-subscribed channel and threw. (The `.on()`→`.subscribe()` order within a single instance was already correct.)

FIX: each instance now uses a UNIQUE channel name `notifications-${user.id}-${instanceId}` (instanceId = a per-mount `useRef` random). Handlers are registered before `.subscribe()` (once), and `removeChannel` cleans up on unmount/user-change. This is the ONLY Realtime channel in the app (verified). RULE: never share a static Realtime channel topic across components that may mount more than once — make the topic unique per instance, and always register all `.on(...)` before `.subscribe()`.

## Full app i18n sweep — dashboards + auth/flow pages now bilingual (COMPLETES the above)

The "STILL Spanish" sweep noted above is now DONE. Everything below was translated to full ES/EN (paired namespaces in `messages/es.json` + `en.json`, each component wired with `useTranslations`/`getTranslations`; `tsc` + `next build` green per commit; pushed to main).

- **Professional dashboard** (`dashboard/profesional` + children): shell/header, `profile-completion`, `profile-editor`, `services-editor`, `availability-editor`, `photo-gallery`, `booking-requests`, `proposals-tab`, `verification-panel`, `unsaved-guard`. Namespaces: `proPanel`, `profileEditor`, `servicesEditor`, `availabilityEditor`, `photoGallery`, `bookingRequests`, `proposalsTab`, `verificationPanel`, `unsavedGuard`.
- **Client dashboard** (`dashboard/cliente` + `client-activity`): `clientPage`, `clientActivity`, `savedPros` (saved-professionals-tab).
- **Account / support**: `account-security` (`accountSecurity`), `close-account-section` (`closeAccount`), `support-tickets` (`supportTickets`), `notifications-list` (extended `notifications` with seeAll/markRead/delete/deleteAll/deleteAllConfirm/noneList).
- **Auth/flow pages**: `registro` (role choice — `registerChoice`), `registro/cliente` (`registerClient`), `onboarding` (`onboarding`, reuses `registerChoice` cards), `completar-perfil` (`completeProfile`), `olvide-contrasena` (`forgotPassword`), `reset-password` (`resetPassword`), `publicar-proyecto` (`publicarProyecto`), `notificaciones` (`notifications`), `proveedores-autorizados` (`proveedoresPage` — converted to server component with `generateMetadata` + `getTranslations`, `t.rich` for bold), `contacto` (`contacto` — hero, react-hook-form validation messages, ticket-success user/guest states, support/hours/location cards).

PATTERNS used throughout: module-level constant label maps (STATUS_LABEL, TIMELINES, FILTERS) moved INSIDE components and converted to `t()` helpers (they need locale context); list-loop variables renamed off `t` to avoid shadowing the translation fn (e.g. `tk` in support-tickets); locale-aware dates via `locale === "en" ? "en-US" : "es-CR"`; taxonomy via `getCategoryLabel(id, locale)`.

**Minor items still Spanish (intentional / low-value, NOT blocking):** a few zod schema validation messages defined at module scope (no locale context); `notificationContextLabel` tags ("Como profesional/cliente"); the `/buscar` strings noted above. Legal pages (`/terminos`, `/privacidad`) and the entire `admin/*` panel remain Spanish by design.

## Routing: middleware → `src/proxy.ts` (Next 16) + universal locale redirect + legacy-route cleanup

**Next 16 renamed the `middleware` file convention to `proxy`** (deprecated `middleware`; see `node_modules/next/dist/docs/01-app/03-api-reference/03-file-conventions/proxy.md`). For a **`src/` project the file must be `src/proxy.ts`** (same level as `src/app`), exporting a function named `proxy` (or default). **The old `middleware.ts` at the repo ROOT silently STOPPED running when the app moved to Next 16** — the entire edge layer was dead: no auth gate, no onboarding redirect, no locale handling. Symptom that proved it: an unauthenticated `GET /es/dashboard/profesional` returned **200** (rendered) instead of redirecting to `/login`. (How it was hidden: the homepage still worked via `src/app/page.tsx`'s `redirect("/es")`, and the 6 legacy routes still worked via their own redirect stubs — so nothing looked obviously broken.)

**Fix (commit bc442c2):**
- Moved the proxy to **`src/proxy.ts`** (function `middleware` → `proxy`, import `./i18n/routing`). The production build now prints **`ƒ Proxy (Middleware)`** — confirmation it's active. **If the build does NOT list `Proxy (Middleware)`, the proxy isn't wired** (wrong filename/location) — that's the canary.
- The proxy now makes **EVERY unprefixed path 307-redirect to its locale-prefixed canonical URL** (`NEXT_LOCALE` cookie → `/en`, else `/es`; params preserved). Previously next-intl's `handleI18n` was NOT redirecting unprefixed paths here, so only the 6 routes that had hand-written redirect stubs worked and **every other non-localized URL 404'd** (`/como-funciona`, `/ayuda`, `/contacto`, `/soporte`, …). 307 (temporary) is deliberate — the target locale depends on the cookie; SEO canonicalization is via page metadata, not the redirect status.
- **Removed the 6 now-redundant legacy redirect-stub pages** (`src/app/buscar`, `/login`, `/registro`, `/registro/cliente`, `/registro/profesional`, `/profesionales/[slug]`). They were NOT duplicate designs (a prior pass had already reduced them to 2-line `redirect()` stubs) — the proxy supersedes them. **Kept** root `src/app/page.tsx` (homepage `/`→`/es` belt-and-suspenders), root `src/app/layout.tsx` (required for root `not-found`/`global-error`), and `src/app/auth/callback` (excluded from the proxy matcher so the OAuth PKCE code survives).
- **The `[locale]` routes are the single source of truth.** Never re-add non-localized page files at the app root — the proxy redirects everything. Internal navigation already uses `@/i18n/navigation` `Link` (auto-prefixed), so only external/bookmarked links rely on the proxy redirect.

**Verified** (dev server, all green): every unprefixed path → 307 `/es/<same path>` (or `/en/…` with the EN cookie); localized targets render 200 (es + en); auth gate restored (`/es/dashboard/*` → `/es/login` when logged out); no redirect loops on already-prefixed paths. `tsc` + `next build` pass.

**Gotcha for future routing work:** Next 16 + Turbopack dev does **not** hot-reload proxy edits, and clearing `.next` is not always enough — fully **restart `next dev`** after touching `src/proxy.ts`, and confirm behavior with `curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code} %{redirect_url}"` against unprefixed + prefixed paths.

## App-wide design-quality initiative — Pass 1: foundation (review outcome)

A multi-pass design-quality sweep is underway (foundation → public pages → auth/registration → pro dashboard → client dashboard → admin). **Pass 1 (foundation)** verified the shared layer rather than rebuilding it:
- **Finding: the unified design system + shared components were already in place** from prior passes. `contratacr-design-guide.md` (now anchored at the top as the authoritative standard) fully documents tokens, primitives, and patterns; the shared primitives (`Button`, `Input`/`Select`, `Badge`, `Card`, `Avatar`), the single app-wide `LandingNavbar` + `LandingFooter`, the mobile drawer, language toggle, account menu, modals (`LoginModal`/`SelfActionModal`), and `NotificationBell` already conform. **No toast library exists by design** — feedback is inline (error/success blocks + the autosave status line).
- **Home (`/`) review (protect-only):** strong and conformant — Hero (rotating headline + search) · `ProsSection` carousel · `WhyContratacr` · `FindByZone` · footer. No redesign; left intact.
- **Changes made:** added the authoritative "unified design system" anchor to the top of the design guide (home = quality bar; canonical kit; inline feedback); fixed a stale `middleware`→`src/proxy.ts` comment in `landing-navbar`.
- **Conclusion driving the next passes:** the real inconsistency is at the **page level** (public pages, auth, dashboards, admin), not the shared kit. Each subsequent pass aligns those screens to the already-established standard — reuse the primitives, kill nested bordered boxes, keep one primary action per screen.

**Pass 3 (public pages) — in progress.** Like the shared kit, the public pages are structurally conformant; the recurring real defect is **EN Spanish-leaks** (hardcoded strings the i18n sweep missed), not layout. Done so far:
- **`/buscar`** (the priority screen) — confirmed structurally on-spec (full-width `max-w-[1920px]` 3-column shell, numbered cards ↔ map pins, empty state, pagination). **Now fully EN-clean across the whole cluster:**
  - Page: pagination (`search.pagination.*`, `pageOf` via `t.rich`); voseo fix (`search.noResults.desc` "expandí"→"amplía").
  - `SearchFilters`: placeholder, "Cercanía", "Aseguradora", "Cualquiera", near-me button + geo errors, "Solo identidad verificada", clear-search/remove-insurer aria-labels → `search.filters.*` / `search.sort.cercania`.
  - `SearchResultsLayout` (shell had NO translator): "Filtros"/"Lista"/"Mapa"/"Cerrar"/"Ver resultados" → `search.list/map/viewResults/close` (Filtros reuses `filters.title`).
  - `ProfessionalCard` coverage labels → `card.coverageCountry/coverageYourLocation/coverageAreas` (helper takes the `card` translator).
  - `ProfessionalSchedule` (had NO translator): day labels, "Próximos horarios", empty state, day-nav aria-labels, "Ver horario completo" ×2, "Solicitar servicio", location aria-label + Videoconsulta/A domicilio/Ubicación labels → new `schedule.*` namespace; **day-column dates now locale-aware via `Intl` (es-CR/en-US)** instead of the Spanish `DAY_SHORT`/`MONTH_SHORT` arrays. "General" stays an internal sentinel.
  - `SelfActionModal`: `SELF_MSG` values became i18n **keys** (resolved inside the modal via `selfAction.messages.<key>`) so all 5 call sites are untouched; title + OK button translated. `save-button` aria-labels → `card.save/unsave`.
- **`ProfessionalSchedule` is reused on the public profile too**, so this also benefits `/profesionales/[slug]`.
- **STILL TODO in Pass 3:** `/profesionales/[slug]` page itself (large — tabs Servicios/Disponibilidad/Casos de éxito/Certificaciones/Reseñas/Sobre mí, review-category labels Precio/Puntualidad/Calidad/Comunicación, "Ver cómo me ven los clientes"/"Volver a mi panel", etc. — still heavily hardcoded Spanish); and the info pages `/categorias`, `/como-funciona`, `/ayuda`, `/atraer-clientes`, `/soporte`, plus legal-page styling. (`/contacto` + `/proveedores-autorizados` were already translated in the earlier i18n sweep.)
- **Method that works:** `grep -noE '"[A-ZÁÉÍÓÚÑ]...' ` the component tree for bare Spanish literals + voseo before assuming a "translated" page is done — leaks hide in aria-labels, select placeholders, geo/error strings, and module-level helper functions/const maps. **Caveat:** that grep misses (a) **lowercase** quoted strings (e.g. `timeAgo` → "hace N días") and (b) JSX text containing `{interpolation}` (the `{` breaks the `[^<>{}]*` class) — check relative-time/format helpers and interpolated headings manually.

**Pass 3 (public pages) — COMPLETE.** Beyond `/buscar`:
- **`/profesionales/[slug]`** fully translated (~50 strings: not-found, WhatsApp prefill, tabs, preview bar, verification badges, all 6 tab bodies, sub-ratings, Sobre-mí cards) + voseo fix ("Contáctalo") + a hardcoded `/es/` link swapped for the i18n `Link`. Child components done: **ReviewSection** (Intl.RelativeTimeFormat for timestamps), **ProfileGallery** (`gallery` ns), **ReportProfileModal** (`report` ns — reason sent to admin stays canonical Spanish while UI shows the translated label).
- **CategorySearch** (`ui/category-search`, reused in /buscar + registration + profile editor) → new `categorySearch` ns (placeholders, no-results, "Otro servicio", the whole suggestion flow).
- **Info pages** (`/categorias`, `/como-funciona`, `/ayuda`, `/atraer-clientes`, `/soporte`, `/contacto`, `/proveedores-autorizados`) were already fully translated (verified clean). **Legal pages** (`/terminos`, `/privacidad`) already match the guide's legal-document spec (draft banner, TOC, scroll-mt sections) — Spanish-only by rule, no change.

New i18n namespaces added this pass: `search.pagination`, extended `search.filters`/`search.sort`/`search.list/map/...`, `card.coverage*`/`card.save/unsave`, `schedule`, `selfAction`, `categorySearch`, `gallery`, `report`, and a large extension of `profile`.

**Pass 4 (auth + registration) — IN PROGRESS.** Done so far:

- **PATTERN for zod + i18n:** module-level `z.object(...)` with hardcoded messages → move the schema **inside** the component in a `useMemo([t])` and use `t(...)` for each message; type the form as a plain `type FormData = {...}` (drop `z.infer`).

All functionality, permissions, audit trail, and the horizontally-scrolling mobile tab bar left intact.

**ALL 7 design-quality passes COMPLETE.** The user-facing app is fully ES/EN bilingual end-to-end; the admin panel is internally consistent (Spanish-only).

**Post-pass cleanup — bilingual data labels.** Closed the last data-translation gap: **languages** now carry a `labelEn` on every `LANGUAGES` entry and resolve via `languageLabel(id, locale)` (mirrors `getCategoryLabel`), so spoken-language chips read "English, French" in EN; the profile-editor `LanguagesInput` autocomplete also matches the English name and renders in the active locale. **Insurers are intentionally NOT translated** — they're brand/proper nouns (INS, MAPFRE, Sagicor, Davivienda Seguros, Oceánica…) that don't localize. So the categories/groups/languages taxonomy is now fully bilingual and no `_EN` work remains.

**Sprint 105 — six targeted UX fixes (registration, disponibilidad, contacto, ubicaciones, panels).** All committed to main, build green (`✓ Compiled successfully` + `ƒ Proxy (Middleware)`), ES/EN both updated.
- **Disponibilidad — cross-location time conflict guard** (`availability-editor.tsx` `insertSlots`). A pro can't be in two places at once: when generating/adding slots, any time already scheduled THAT date at a DIFFERENT `location_id` (any profession) is detected, skipped, and surfaced inline via new `availabilityEditor.errLocationConflict` ("No puedes agregar la misma hora en dos ubicaciones. Ya tienes las {time} en {location} ese día."). Non-conflicting times still insert; the conflict check sits right after the lead-time filter.
- **Registro profesional — two UI fixes** (`registro/profesional/page.tsx`). (1) The "No tengo identificación costarricense" option is now a **subtle disclosure link** (`NoCrIdToggle` rewritten as a text button) anchored DIRECTLY under the identification field in BOTH the email step and the OAuth step — removed the floating checkbox at the end of the form. New keys `registration.pro.noCrIdLink`/`hasCrIdLink`. (2) "Agregar otra profesión" is now an explicit **"+ Agregar otra profesión"** action (`showExtraProf` state) that reveals the picker on click — no more vague second dropdown. Added `autoFocus` prop to `CategorySearch` (opens the picker on mount when revealed).
- **Contacto — progressive call-number disclosure** (`profile-editor.tsx`). WhatsApp field → "Permitir contacto por llamada" toggle → the "Número para llamadas" field is revealed ONLY when the toggle is ON (empty = WhatsApp used for calls). Removed `whatsappHelp`, `allowCallDesc`, and the `secContactDesc` ("Cómo te contactan los clientes") subtitle + the `emailHelp` text. (2) "Finalizada"/completed bookings switched from the prominent brand-blue `default` Badge to the neutral **`muted`** variant (client-activity.tsx + booking-requests.tsx) so the terminal status reads consistent with the other statuses (no standout box).

Both linked from the footer (already), pro registration (already), **and now** `registro/cliente` + the client-registration-modal (both reference Términos + Política de Privacidad).
- **Badge → "Verificado"** everywhere it's the chip (cards/profile/panels/booking), ES+EN ("Verified"); explanatory copy keeps fuller wording. **Meaning always accessible:** a "¿Qué significa?" link sits beside the badge on the public profile (header + "Sobre mí" row) → `/proveedores-autorizados#que-significa`, whose disclaimer (anchor added) states the LIMITED meaning (only cédula-real + name-match; NOT quality/outcome/completion/permits/physical identity). Cards keep the title tooltip + link through to the profile.
- **Registration with OR without cédula** (`registro/profesional`): the "Registrarme sin cédula por ahora" choice registers a pro unverified (no badge), verifiable later from the panel. Backend already **skips** verification for a null cédula (status stays `pending` default — confirmed in migration 023; `runIdentityVerification` returns "skipped", never rejects). This path is a NORMAL unverified signup → payload `noCrId: false` (only "¿No es tu información?"/`identityMismatch` still routes to review). Simplified the no-cédula step to a name field + a short "no badge" note (dropped the foreigner notice + document textarea + `idDocNote` state). Minors handled via Terms acceptance at registration (no age checkbox).
- **/buscar card fixes:** (a) **uniform mobile height** — the action zone now has `min-h-[150px] md:min-h-0` so all 3 button-layout types reserve the same space and cards line up on mobile; (b) **"Ver horario completo" restored** as a right-aligned link sharing the schedule's existing header row (opens booking; no new line, no size increase); (c) **reviews are now a link** — rating+count on cards links to `/profesionales/[slug]?tab=resenas` (count brand-blue, hover underline; ICU plural "1 reseña"/"2 reseñas"); the 0-review state stays the honest non-link "Sin reseñas todavía"; (d) "Escribir por correo" → **"Contactar por correo"** (EN "Contact by email"); (e) **aseguradora filter** starts unfiltered (placeholder "Cualquiera"), and the option list drops any "Ninguna/Todas/sin seguros" entry by id AND label.

**Sprint 108 — contact/email toggles, "Sobre mí" + client-panel structure, tiquete wording, ONE save pattern, /buscar cards.** All on main, build green, ES/EN updated.
- **Contacto + Disponibilidad** (`profile-editor.tsx`, `availability-editor.tsx`): Contacto subsection regained its subtitle (`secContactDesc`); "Correo de contacto" became an opt-in toggle (`showContactEmail`, off unless one is saved; turning off clears it) mirroring the call-number pattern; removed the "Cómo te contactan los clientes" heading from Disponibilidad (the saving spinner moved next to the privacy toggle).
- **"Sobre mí" redesign** (`profesionales/[slug]/page.tsx`): bio is the lead paragraph; every fact (Experiencia, Verificación, Ubicación, Precios, Lugares de trabajo, Idiomas, Aseguradoras) shares ONE uniform `<dl>` of label/value rows with hairline dividers — no per-item boxes, no mismatched headings; multi-value rows stack right-aligned.
- **Client panel restructure** (`dashboard/cliente/page.tsx`): "Mi perfil" is now one titled `Card` with divider-separated subsections (foto, datos, "Ofrecer mis servicios"); **"Cuenta y seguridad" is its own nav tab** (Settings icon, `tabAccount`) holding AccountSecuritySection(showHeading=false)+CloseAccountSection — mirrors the pro panel.
- **i18n text fixes**: em-dashes→commas in `passwordProviderIntro` (ES+EN); Spanish support UI "tickets"→**"tiquetes"** (values only — keys + English keep "ticket"); `support.yourConversations` → "Tus tiquetes de soporte." / "Your support tickets.".
- **ONE app-wide SAVE pattern = AUTOSAVE everywhere** (see design-guide §21). New shared `<SaveStatus>` (`components/dashboard/save-status.tsx`, `saveStatus` namespace: Guardando…/Guardado/Sin guardar) shown top-right of every editable section. Removed the "Guardar cambios" buttons from Pro Mi perfil and Client profile (both now debounce-autosave 1.5s + keep the `UnsavedChangesGuard`); Servicios/Disponibilidad/Photos already persist per action and now show the same indicator (Disponibilidad/Photos pulse "Guardado"). Rationale: the app was already autosave-dominant and Disponibilidad/Photos are action-based (can't sit behind a deferred button), so autosave is the only truly consistent option.
- **/buscar cards** (`professional-card.tsx`, `professional-schedule.tsx`, `search-filters.tsx`) — reorganized WITHIN the existing `md:min-h-[190px]` floor (no size increase): contact + primary actions grouped in the action zone in **3 layouts** — TYPE 1 (public+call) `[WhatsApp · Llamar]` row then Solicitar servicio; TYPE 2 (public+no call) `[WhatsApp]` then Solicitar; TYPE 3 (private) only WhatsApp (+Llamar if enabled), no schedule. In TYPE 1/2 WhatsApp/Llamar are side-by-side on ONE line (above Solicitar) so adding Llamar never adds height; in **TYPE 3 they STACK vertically** (WhatsApp above Llamar, `w-full`, bottom-pinned) since they're the only actions — `contactButtons(stacked)` switches column/`w-full` vs row/`flex-1`. Card height unchanged either way (min-h floor). Price moved to the top row beside the name as just "₡X/hora" (no Desde/Tarifa; hidden when non-numeric to avoid an EN leak); "Identidad verificada"→compact "Verificado" (icon+short text, `verifiedShort`) next to the name; removed the top-row `TopContactIcons` (now unused), the "Próximos horarios" label, and the redundant "Ver horario completo" link. Schedule columns always show the full date ("15 mar"/"Mar 15"), never "lun 15". Kept casos/cert counts. Aseguradora filter defensively drops any "ninguna/sin seguros" option; default = no insurer (placeholder "Cualquiera").

**Sprint 107 — optional map pin is a clean opt-in disclosure** (`workplaces-picker.tsx`). The exact-pin refinement (Places address search + map) is **hidden by default** and revealed by a lightweight expandable LINK ("Buscar la dirección y marcar el punto en el mapa") — `MapPin` + label + `ChevronDown`, brand-blue, `aria-expanded` — replacing the previous heavy bordered disclosure row (de-boxed per design §19). `showMap` still defaults to `false`, and the map only mounts when toggled on (the `[showMap]` effect inits/【resets `mapInstanceRef`】), so the section is just provincia/cantón until opt-in (compact on mobile). A `Check` shows on the collapsed toggle when a pin is already set. The SAME `WorkplacesPicker` drives BOTH registration (`registro/profesional`) and the panel (`profile-editor`, `mapHeight={168}`), so the control is identical in both — no per-flow variance. **Location logic unchanged:** provincia/cantón remain authoritative for `/buscar` (`computeSearchAreas` → `search_provincias`/`search_cantones`); the pin only stores `lat`/`lng`; Places search (new `PlaceAutocompleteElement` → legacy `Autocomplete` fallback) intact, so a pro saved with OR without a pin still filters correctly. (`LocationPicker` is a separate component used elsewhere, e.g. publicar-proyecto — untouched.)

**Sprint 110 — card/filter polish, badge simplification, dashboard nav, categories + health DOB.**
- **/buscar cards** (`professional-card.tsx`, `professional-schedule.tsx`): "Ver horario completo" moved from the top-right header row to a centered line BELOW the schedule grid (was overlapping the card bookmark on desktop; now matches the requested mobile placement). Reviews row: only the "N reseñas" count is the link (star + rating value are plain text). Casos de éxito / Certificaciones links dropped their `(N)` counts (`viewCases`/`viewCertifications` no longer take `{count}`); certifications link stays conditional. Card gained a mobile height floor `min-h-[320px]` (desktop keeps `md:min-h-[190px]`) for uniform mobile cards. `card.email`/`profile.email` → "Contactar por correo".
- **"Verificado" badge simplified**: removed the ShieldCheck icon from EVERY badge (search card, public profile header + Sobre mí, pro dashboard Badge, profile editor, client account, proveedores-autorizados hero chip, landing phone-screen mockup) — text-only now. Removed the inline "¿Qué significa?" links beside the profile badges (explanation still on `/proveedores-autorizados` + Terms). Swept remaining "identidad verificada"/"Identity verified" copy → "Verificado"/"Verified" (filters, FAQ/tips, verify benefit, notifications, `verificationLabel`).
- **/buscar filters** (`search-filters.tsx`, `search-results-layout.tsx`, `ui/select.tsx`): aseguradora placeholder is now the neutral field label "Aseguradora"/"Insurer" (was "Cualquiera"/"Any"); default unselected = unfiltered, X clears. **Single behavior = instant-apply everywhere**; removed the redundant mobile drawer "Ver resultados" apply button (X + backdrop dismiss). Select trigger uses `focus-visible` (not `focus`) with a brand-blue ring so a mouse click that returns focus to the trigger no longer leaves a STUCK border.
- **Soporte/tiquetes** (`terminos/page.tsx`, `soporte/page.tsx`): Terms footer offers support only via the Soporte ticket channel ("Contáctanos desde Soporte.") + keeps the Privacy link (dropped the raw mailto). Attach button "Adjuntar imagen o PDF" → "Adjuntar imagen o documento" (EN "Attach image or document"). Support ticket **name field is now REQUIRED** (auto-fills for logged-in users).
- **Public profile** (`profesionales/[slug]`): "Sobre mí" redesigned to a left-aligned label-over-value GRID (2 cols sm+, uppercase muted labels, no per-row dividers; Precios + Lugares span full width); only one hairline separates the bio. Removed the redundant "Llamar" from the Disponibilidad tab (both branches) — it already lives in the sidebar contact card.
- **Dashboard spacing** (`dashboard/{cliente,profesional}/page.tsx`): root-caused the big title↔content gap — `CardContent` kept its default `pt-5` because the override only set `px-6 pb-6`. Now `CardHeader pb-3` + `CardContent pt-1` = consistent ~16px everywhere. Client "Mi perfil" content gap 6→4. Home category carousel already links each card to `/buscar?categoria=<id>` (verified — no change). Removed the registration add-photo caption. Client account name help reworded: "Tu nombre proviene de la cédula que ingresaste en tu última solicitud de servicio…".
- **Completa tu perfil** (`profile-completion.tsx`, `profile-editor.tsx`, `dashboard/profesional/page.tsx`, `globals.css`): completion items now OPEN the matching collapsed section in the profile editor AND smooth-scroll to the exact field with a brief brand-blue `.field-flash`. ProfileEditor sections are now CONTROLLED (open state lifted; `focusField`/`focusKey` props; `FIELD_SECTION` map; `data-field` anchors on photo/bio/location/whatsapp/insurers). Description completion now counts ANY non-empty bio (removed the ≥40-char gate).
- **Admin user view** (`admin-user-profile.tsx`, `api/admin/users/route.ts`, `admin-users.tsx`): phone now resolves from the professional record (added `whatsapp`/`call_phone`/`allow_phone_call` to the API + type) — a pro shows "WhatsApp: … · Llamada: …" (separate call line when set, else the WhatsApp number for both); clients show `profiles.phone`; numbers prettified. Search helper reworded so it no longer feels contradictory (search by name/email/full cédula; note clarifies the cédula is always shown masked). Cédula stays masked server-side via `maskId` (unchanged).
- **Categories** (`categories.ts`, migration **052_more_categories.sql — apply in Supabase**): completed health set (added **Odontología, Pediatría, Optometría** as es_salud; relabeled `medicina_domicilio` → "Medicina general") and added new hireable services — **Chef privado y cocina** (eventos), **Servicio de gas** + **Reparación de electrodomésticos** (hogar), **Entrenamiento deportivo** + **Cuido y paseo de mascotas** (salud group, NOT es_salud). EN labels for all. Taxonomy is centralized so the megamenu, /categorias, /buscar filter, project + profession selectors and "¿no ves tu categoría?" all update automatically. `category_id` FK was already dropped (013) so existing data is safe. (Home carousel left as its curated image-backed subset.)
- **Health booking DOB autocomplete** (migration **053_padron_dob.sql — apply in Supabase**, `identity-verifier.ts`): root cause was the DATA layer — the `padron` table/RPC carried no birth date, so the verifier always returned `dob:null` and bookings always asked DOB manually. The route → verifier → booking auto-fill plumbing was already correct (self field locks, beneficiary prefills, for both self + third-party). Fix (server-side only, padrón stays private, NO auth gate, verification flow untouched): added `padron.fecha_nacimiento` (+ staging + swap fn), `padron_lookup` returns it, verifier returns DOB (YYYY-MM-DD) + derives `isAdult`. DOB autocompletes once a DOB-bearing dataset is loaded; the bare TSE electoral roll has none → manual fallback (and cédulas not in the padrón still ask manually).

**Sprint 111 — "free forever" copy removed, subscription infra (inactive), padrón size analysis.**
- **No permanent-free promise** (`messages/{es,en}.json`): reworded the only strings that promised perpetual/total free use — `footer.tagline`, `comoFunciona.faq0A`, `ayuda.faq0A` — to honest present-tense ("Hoy crear tu cuenta y empezar es gratis", "No cobramos comisiones por los servicios"). Dropped "completamente gratuito / gratuito sin cargos de ningún tipo / no cobramos mensualidades / sin excepción" (EN mirrored). Action CTAs ("Regístrate gratis", etc.) kept — they describe the free action now, not perpetual free. No future paid plans announced anywhere.
- **Professional subscription infrastructure — BUILT BUT INACTIVE** (everything gated by `PAYMENTS_ENABLED`, default OFF). Prices ₡3.000/mes, ₡30.000/año (2 meses gratis).
  - **Flag**: `src/lib/payments/config.ts` → `PAYMENTS_ENABLED = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_PAYMENTS_ENABLED === "true"` (default false; NEXT_PUBLIC so client hides UI + server gates logic). Also PRICES/CYCLE_MONTHS/CURRENCY/SINPE_PAYMENT placeholder/formatColones.
  - **DB (migration 054 — apply in Supabase)**: `subscriptions` (one row per pro: plan free/premium, status active/inactive/expired/pending/cancelled, billing_cycle, price_paid ₡, payment_method, started_at, current_period_end, gateway refs, card_last4) + `subscription_payments` ledger. **RLS = owner-read only; all writes via service-role** (admin APIs / webhooks). **NEVER stores card data** — gateway references only.
  - **Backend** (`src/lib/payments/subscriptions.ts`, server-only via admin client): isSubscriptionActive, computePeriodEnd (monthly/annual), priceForCycle, getSubscription/getPayments/ensureSubscription, **activatePaidPeriod** (writes payment + sets active + extends period; used by manual SINPE today and the gateway webhook later), setSubscriptionStatus.
  - **Gateway stub** (`src/lib/payments/gateway.ts`): `PaymentGateway` interface + `StubGateway` (throws "not integrated"); swap point for ONVO/Tilopay. Raw card data never touches our server/DB.
  - **APIs**: `/api/payments/subscription` (pro self GET → enabled:false while off; POST card checkout → 404/501 while off/no gateway), `/api/payments/webhook` (inert until wired), `/api/admin/subscriptions` (admin-only: GET status+history, POST activate/extend/deactivate — the manual SINPE flow).
  - **Pro UI (hidden)**: `subscription-panel.tsx` mounted ONLY via a "Suscripción" dashboard tab that exists ONLY when `PAYMENTS_ENABLED` (GROUP_PRO conditionally includes it; content branch double-guarded). With the flag off there is no tab, no link, no content — the app is identical to the free version for normal users.
  - **Admin UI (visible now, admin-only)**: `AdminSubscription` in the admin user profile — status, payment history, and manual "marcar pago SINPE recibido y activar/extender/desactivar".
  - **To activate later**: apply migration 054, implement a gateway in `gateway.ts` (+ webhook signature verify), set the real `SINPE_PAYMENT` number, update the legal pages (NOT touched here), set `NEXT_PUBLIC_PAYMENTS_ENABLED=true`.
- **Padrón storage analysis (report only — no changes)**: the `padron` table holds the full TSE electoral roll (~3.6M rows) with ONLY `cedula` + 3 name fields (+ the new nullable `fecha_nacimiento`, empty until a DOB dataset is loaded — negligible while NULL). Estimated footprint ≈ heap ~250–300 MB + PK(cedula) index ~90–140 MB ≈ **~350–400 MB total** → it is almost certainly the dominant consumer and would nearly fill the **free 500 MB** tier by itself; on **Pro (8 GB)** it's ~5% and not a concern. `padron_staging` is a temp load table left EMPTY after `finalize_padron_swap()` (load→swap→recreate empty), and `padron_old` is dropped in the swap — so no duplicate at rest. Data is already minimized (4 cols, single PK index = the lookup index). Options if footprint must shrink (none destructive applied): confirm/`TRUNCATE padron_staging` if a load was interrupted; keep only cedula+name(+dob) (already done); cannot trim to "only queried" rows (any cédula may register, so the full roll is required); the full national roll is inherent — best mitigation is staying on Pro, or moving the padrón to an external verification provider (the `IdentityVerifier` abstraction already supports swapping) to remove it from the app DB. **Exact sizes**: run in the Supabase SQL Editor — `select relname, pg_size_pretty(pg_total_relation_size(c.oid)) total, pg_size_pretty(pg_indexes_size(c.oid)) idx, (select reltuples::bigint from pg_class where oid=c.oid) rows from pg_class c join pg_namespace n on n.oid=c.relnamespace where n.nspname='public' and c.relkind='r' order by pg_total_relation_size(c.oid) desc;` and `select pg_size_pretty(pg_database_size(current_database()));`.

**Sprint 112 — admin-only subscription preview route (still inactive).** Built the `/admin/suscripciones` server-guarded preview (option b) so admins can see the real pro-facing plans page before launch, without exposing anything to regular users.
- **Route** `src/app/[locale]/admin/suscripciones/page.tsx`: `requireAdmin()` (non-admins redirected to the admin login → unreachable by URL), inside `AdminShell` (new "Suscripciones" nav tab, `CreditCard` icon). Shows a flag-state + plans banner and renders the REAL pro-facing `SubscriptionPanel` in preview. Pointer to Usuarios → pro → Suscripción for per-pro management.
- **Self-API** `/api/payments/subscription` GET: now `getApiAdmin()` first — an ADMIN caller is treated as `enabled` (returns `preview:true` while `PAYMENTS_ENABLED` off) so the preview renders even if the admin has no pro profile (subscription null, payments []); **non-admins still get `{ enabled:false }` with no data**. Card POST still 404 for everyone while off (no real checkout; gateway stub).
- **Reset action**: `deleteSubscription(professionalId)` (deletes the subscription row + its payment ledger) → `/api/admin/subscriptions` action `"reset"` + an "Eliminar / reset" button in `AdminSubscription`, so preview/testing never leaves rows on a real professional.
- **Flag-off confirmed**: with `PAYMENTS_ENABLED=false` (default) every pro keeps full free access, regular users see ZERO subscription/payment UI (no dashboard tab, self-API leaks nothing), and the ONLY visible surfaces are admin-only (the preview route + the admin user-profile Suscripción card). No card data stored; card stays behind the gateway stub.

**Migration 054 — how to apply (Supabase SQL Editor):** open the Supabase dashboard → SQL Editor → paste the full contents of `supabase/migrations/054_subscriptions.sql` → Run. It's idempotent (IF NOT EXISTS / DROP POLICY IF EXISTS) so it's safe to re-run. It creates `subscriptions` + `subscription_payments` with owner-read RLS (writes are service-role only). Nothing activates from this — the feature stays behind `PAYMENTS_ENABLED`.

**Sprint 113 — customer-facing plans page + admin payment-approval flow (still inactive behind PAYMENTS_ENABLED).**
- **Plans page redesigned** (`subscription-panel.tsx`, modern SaaS style): two plan cards — **Anual ₡30.000** (highlighted "2 meses gratis", equivalence /mes shown) and **Mensual ₡3.000** — benefits list, then a **payment-method step**: OPTION 1 **Tarjeta** (primary; behind the gateway stub → shows "disponible pronto" and falls back to SINPE, no live processing), OPTION 2 **SINPE Móvil o transferencia** (manual) → SINPE instructions (number/amount/reference) + **comprobante upload (image/PDF)** + submit. Shows an "en revisión" banner while a payment is pending and an active-plan summary when premium. Responsive, Spanish-only (pending/internal surface). Same ONE component is the real pro page when the flag flips on.
- **Comprobante upload**: `/api/payments/receipt` → Cloudinary `contratacr/comprobantes` (`resource_type: auto` → image + PDF), auth required, gated (PAYMENTS_ENABLED or admin).
- **Manual submit**: `/api/payments/subscription/manual` → `submitManualPayment()` creates a **PENDING** payment (grants nothing; supersedes a prior pending one) and marks the subscription `pending`.
- **Admin approval (visible now, admin-only)** on **/admin/suscripciones**: `AdminPaymentApprovals` lists pending SINPE/transfer payments with the comprobante link; **Aprobar y activar** → `approveManualPayment()` marks the same payment row paid + activates/extends the subscription (status active + expiry) automatically; **Rechazar** → `rejectManualPayment()` (kept in history with reason, reverts to free if not otherwise active). PATCH verb on `/api/admin/subscriptions`. The existing per-pro manual activate + **Eliminar / reset** remain in the admin user-profile card.
- **DB migration 055 (apply AFTER 054)**: `subscription_payments` gains `receipt_url`, `reviewed_by`, `reviewed_at`, a `'rejected'` status (CHECK extended), and a partial pending index.
- **Flag-off still clean**: regular users see zero subscription UI; the plans page is reachable only via the admin preview; card data never stored (gateway-only).

**Sprint 114 — admin smart search, unverified label removed, no-schedule WhatsApp, uniform cards, filter labels.**
- **Admin user search** (`api/admin/users` GET search): word-based, any-order partial matching — every query word must appear somewhere in the full name (PostgREST `and(full_name.ilike.%w1%,full_name.ilike.%w2%)`), so "Isaac Sanchez" finds "Isaac Alberto Sanchez Monge". Email matches the whole query; cédula partial. Results ranked in JS (accent-insensitive): contiguous full match > word-start > partial; limit 50; input sanitized of or()/and() grammar chars.
- **Unverified pros show NOTHING** (client-facing): removed the unverified badge from the search card, the public-profile header + the "Sobre mí" Verificación row (now only rendered for verified), and the booking-modal "…aún no ha verificado…coordina con cuidado" notice. Only verified pros show "Verificado". The explanation on /proveedores-autorizados + Terms is untouched. Pro's OWN dashboard verification panel/path kept.
- **No published schedule → WhatsApp** (`professional-schedule.tsx`, `booking-modal.tsx`): a public pro with no upcoming slots no longer shows the dead "Solicita el servicio" (would open an empty calendar). Card shows "Este profesional coordina por WhatsApp." + WhatsApp(+Llamar) bottom-pinned (new `schedule.coordinateWhatsApp`). The booking modal's public "no availability" state now shows a "Coordinar por WhatsApp" button (matching the private state), so the profile path never lands on an empty calendar.
- **/buscar cards truly uniform** (`professional-card.tsx`): switched from a min-height floor to a FIXED height (`h-[360px]` mobile, `md:h-[232px]` desktop) + `overflow-hidden`; identity content top-aligned (flex-1), contact/primary buttons bottom-anchored — every card type (WhatsApp-only / WhatsApp+Llamar / schedule / no-slots) is identical height with buttons on the same baseline. Heights are tunable if a future layout needs more room. Name still clamps (never truncated mid-flow).
- **/buscar filter labels + grouping** (`search-filters.tsx`): provincia placeholder unified to "Todas las provincias" (was "Todas"); aseguradora default "Cualquier aseguradora" / "Any insurer", greyed like a placeholder = clearly non-filtering (selecting filters, X clears). Kept TOGGLES for booleans (cerca de mí, Solo verificados) + DROPDOWNS for value choices — grouped under a "Más filtros" heading. focus-visible rings (no stuck border); "Limpiar (N)" now counts every active filter.

**Sprint 115 — honest subscription benefits.** Reframed the plans-page benefit list (`subscription-panel.tsx`, behind PAYMENTS_ENABLED) to match our model: the subscription keeps a professional ACTIVE and able to receive clients — not cosmetic extras. Removed "Apareces destacado en los resultados de búsqueda" (search is filter-based; no featured placement) and "Insignia de plan profesional" (the only badge is "Verificado"). New list: (1) tu perfil aparece en las búsquedas y los clientes pueden encontrarte, (2) recibe solicitudes de servicio de tus clientes, (3) recibe y responde proyectos publicados por clientes, (4) mantén tu perfil profesional activo para seguir recibiendo clientes. Hero reworded "Hazte profesional destacado / Más visibilidad…" → "Mantén tu perfil profesional activo / … para aparecer en las búsquedas y recibir clientes." (Panel stays Spanish-only — admin-preview/pending surface.)

**Sprint 116 — Verificado pill position/style on /buscar cards.** On the search card the "Verificado" mark now uses the SAME visual as the professional dashboard (`Badge variant="verified"` — solid brand-blue pill) and sits to the RIGHT of the name on the name line (name `flex-1 truncate` one line; pill + optional price `shrink-0` on the right), instead of wrapping below the name. Freeing that space, the location + "se desplaza" coverage lines now WRAP in full (removed `truncate`, icon `mt-0.5` top-aligned) so e.g. "La Unión, Cartago +2" / "Se desplaza a tu ubicación." are never cut off on mobile (~360px). Card height unchanged (fixed `h-[360px]` / `md:h-[232px]`). Only `professional-card.tsx` touched.

**Sprint 117 — Verificado pill beside name (desktop) + full name on mobile (cards).** Made the /buscar card name line responsive (`professional-card.tsx`): DESKTOP (`md:flex-nowrap items-center`) the name takes only its content width (`min-w-0`, `md:line-clamp-1` truncates only very long names) so the "Verificado" pill sits IMMEDIATELY beside it; price pushed right via `md:ml-auto`. MOBILE (`flex-wrap items-start`) the name shows in FULL up to 2 lines (`line-clamp-2`, fixing the 1-line truncation from Sprint 116) and the pill wraps to the next line when there's no room. Pill keeps the dashboard blue-pill style; card height unchanged (fixed `h-[360px]` / `md:h-[232px]`). Location lines still wrap in full (Sprint 116). Only the card touched.

**Sprint 118 — shorter person name on /buscar cards.** Added a card-local `shortPersonName()` (`professional-card.tsx`) that shows a PERSON's name as first name + both surnames, dropping middle name(s): "Isaac Alberto Sanchez Monge" → "Isaac Sanchez Monge" (≤3 parts unchanged; rule = `parts.length<=3 ? all : [first, parts[n-2], parts[n-1]]`). Applied to the person name whether it's the primary line (no company) or the subtitle under a company; a **company/brand name is shown verbatim** (company-vs-name rule unchanged). The FULL official name still shows on the professional **profile** (not touched). Frees space and reduces truncation; Verificado pill placement + responsive name wrapping (Sprint 117) and the fixed card height are unchanged. Edge note: the simple first+last-two rule can mishandle particle surnames ("de la Cruz") — acceptable per the requested behavior.

**Sprint 119 — clearer no-schedule message on /buscar cards (per case).** Replaced the single `schedule.coordinateWhatsApp` ("Este profesional coordina por WhatsApp.") with two accurate, concise notes (`professional-schedule.tsx`): (a) **private availability** (hidden on purpose, `!availabilityPublic`) → `availabilityHiddenNote` "Su disponibilidad no es pública. Escríbele para conocer sus horarios." — now also shown on the private card (fills its empty space, no growth); (b) **no schedule posted** (public, no upcoming slots) → `noScheduleNote` "Aún no publica sus horarios. Escríbele para coordinar." A WhatsApp-only-preference card still shows just the contact buttons (no note). Both notes precede the stacked WhatsApp/Llamar buttons. EN added naturally. Card stays fixed height (`h-[360px]`/`md:h-[232px]`) — messages fit existing space. Only the card touched.

**Sprint 120 — filter borders, all filters visible, no-schedule message tweak.**
- **Filter select border fixed** (`search-filters.tsx`): added a filter-only `FILTER_TRIGGER` class (`focus-visible:ring-0 focus-visible:border-[#e5e7eb]`) on the provincia/cantón/orden/aseguradora triggers so the shared Select's brand-blue focus-visible ring no longer "sticks" after selecting (Radix returns focus to the trigger → the ring looked like a stuck colored border). Neutral border in every state; the open dropdown + chevron are the affordance. (Shared `ui/select.tsx` untouched — override is scoped to the filters.)
- **All filters shown directly**: removed the "Más filtros" heading — "Buscar profesionales cerca de mí" and "Solo verificados" toggles now sit at the same level as the dropdowns (only a thin divider separates them). Dropped the unused `filters.moreFilters` key.
- **No-schedule card messages** reworded (`professional-schedule.tsx` keys): private availability → `availabilityHiddenNote` "Disponibilidad privada. Contáctanos para coordinar." (EN "Private availability. Contact us to coordinate."); no schedule posted → `noScheduleNote` "Aún no publica sus horarios. Escríbele para coordinar." (EN "They haven't posted times yet. Message them to coordinate."). Short — card height unchanged.

**Sprint 121 — optional social-media links on the profile + reworded redundant line.**
- **Social links** (URLs only, no storage cost; ADDITIVE to "casos de éxito" photos — both can be used). DB **migration 056** adds `professionals.social_links jsonb` (apply in Supabase). Profile editor gains a **"Redes sociales"** collapsible section (`profile-editor.tsx`) with Instagram / Facebook / TikTok / Sitio web inputs (all optional). Inputs are normalized to full URLs by `socialUrl()` (accepts a full link, `domain/path`, or a bare `@handle`/username → platform URL) and saved in their OWN best-effort update (non-fatal if the column isn't migrated); only filled-in links are stored. Public profile (`profesionales/[slug]`) shows a clean centered row of recognizable social icon buttons (only the ones set) opening in a new tab, in the contact sidebar. Query (`professionals.ts`) exposes `socialLinks` (new `SocialLinks` type, best-effort fetch). Brand icons live in `components/icons/social-icons.tsx` (Instagram/Facebook/TikTok — lucide no longer ships Instagram/Facebook; website uses lucide `Globe`).
- **Text reword**: `opt1Step2Sub` "Fotos de trabajos anteriores, reseñas de clientes reales y cédula verificada." → **"Casos de éxito, reseñas de clientes reales y cédula verificada."** (EN "Success stories, real client reviews, and verified ID.") — uses "casos de éxito" without the redundant "de trabajos anteriores".
- Repo hygiene: `.gitmsg.tmp` added to `.gitignore` (it was being re-tracked by `git add -A`).

**Sprint 122 — nav bugs + verification/step-3 copy.**
- **Publish-project no-flash auth**: `/publicar-proyecto` was a client component that rendered the form then `useEffect`-redirected logged-out users (flash). Split: `page.tsx` is now a SERVER component that checks `safeGetUser` and `redirect(\`/${locale}/login\`)` BEFORE render; the interactive form moved to `publish-form.tsx` (`PublishProjectForm`). No flash.
- **/ayuda cards navigate**: 3 of the 6 category cards had `href:null` (non-clickable divs). The FAQ accordion is now controlled at page level (`openFaq`); each category card leads to its ANSWER — informational cards `goToFaq(i)` (open + smooth-scroll to `#faq-i`, `scroll-mt-24`), the support card links to `/soporte`. Mapping: Crear cuenta→faq1, Buscar→faq3, Verificación→faq2, Solicitudes/proyectos→faq6, Reseñas→faq5, Soporte→/soporte.
- **Verification copy → "Verificado"-only model** (unverified pros are NOT labeled): reworded `comoFunciona.faq1A`, `ayuda.faq2A`, `verificationPanel.noCrUnverifiedBody`/`pendingBody` to drop "Identidad sin verificar" label mentions ("verified show the badge; others simply don't, no negative label"); `proPanel.identityUnverified` dropped the now-false "· visible para clientes". Em-dashes "—" replaced with commas in these strings. EN mirrored. Meaning kept accurate (Verificado = real, verifiable identity via TSE padrón; not work quality).
- **Step 3 accuracy** (`opt1Step3Title/Sub`): "Agenda y coordina directamente" / "Contacto directo sin intermediarios: agenda en la plataforma o coordina por WhatsApp, y acuerdan precio, fecha y horario." — no longer implies price/date/schedule are WhatsApp-exclusive. EN mirrored.
- **Header de-dup**: "Cómo funciona" was both a standalone header link and in Recursos. Removed the standalone; it now lives ONLY in Recursos (desktop dropdown + mobile), one place, no mobile loss.

**Sprint 123 — social links are USERNAME-only (we build the URL).** Refined the Sprint 121 social-links feature: the pro enters ONLY a username, the app constructs the link; we store the clean username (not a URL). New shared `src/lib/social.ts`: `SOCIAL_NETWORKS` (Instagram, Facebook, TikTok, each with a display `prefix`), `cleanUsername()` (lenient — strips `@`, `www.`, or a pasted full URL down to the handle), `isValidUsername()` (`[A-Za-z0-9._-]{1,40}`), `buildSocialUrl(network, username)`. Editor "Redes sociales": each network is a username field with a visible prefix (`instagram.com/`, `facebook.com/`, `tiktok.com/@`) + inline "Usuario no válido"; saves only valid, non-empty clean usernames. Dropped the website field (not a username) and the old URL helper. Public profile builds the URL via `buildSocialUrl` and shows icons only (open in new tab), only for networks filled in. `SocialLinks` type is now usernames (no website); legacy URL values are cleaned to usernames on load/display. No schema change (`social_links` jsonb reused). New i18n: `socialUserPlaceholder`, `socialInvalid`; reworded `secSocialDesc`/`socialHelp` (username framing). No feeds/OAuth/Meta API.

**Sprint 124 — fix false "Guardado" on social links.** Root cause: in `profile-editor.tsx` the `social_links` write was a separate `update` whose error was IGNORED, and `setSaved(true)` ran unconditionally — so when the write failed (most commonly the `social_links` column missing because **migration 056 wasn't applied** → PGRST204 swallowed) the UI showed "Guardado" but nothing persisted (hence it never appeared on the profile). The `professionals` UPDATE RLS is row-level (proven by other identity fields saving), so it's not the blocker. Fix: check the social write result — when the pro is saving usernames (`social_links` non-empty) and it fails, `throw t("socialSaveError")` so the status shows a real error instead of a false "Guardado"; "Guardado" now only shows on genuine success (empty social_links still ignores a missing column so non-social pros aren't blocked). Applies to all networks. **The actual persistence enabler is applying migration 056** (`ALTER TABLE professionals ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS social_links jsonb; NOTIFY pgrst,'reload schema';`) in the Supabase SQL Editor; 056's comment updated (usernames, not URLs). New i18n `profileEditor.socialSaveError` (ES/EN).

**Sprint 125 — diagnose the social-save FAILURE + friendlier subtitle.** Follow-up to 124: the pro now saw the real error `socialSaveError` on save. **Proven root cause** (queried the live PostgREST with the service_role key from `.env.local`, read-only): `GET /rest/v1/professionals?select=social_links` returns Postgres **`42703: column professionals.social_links does not exist`** — the column was genuinely never created, i.e. **migration 056 has NOT been applied** to production. By contrast `select=certifications` returns 200 (that column exists, which is why certs save), confirming it's not RLS/validation/payload/endpoint — those are all correct. (Side finding: `select=fecha_nacimiento` also 42703 → **migration 053 likewise unapplied**, so health-booking DOB autocomplete is also dormant; out of scope here but flagged.) Conclusion: **no code change can make the save persist — the only fix is running migration 056** in the Supabase SQL Editor (`ALTER TABLE public.professionals ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS social_links jsonb; NOTIFY pgrst,'reload schema';`); DDL can't be run through PostgREST/anon/service_role. Once applied, the existing 124 code path saves and persists with no further change. **Subtitle reworded** (`secSocialDesc`, both locales): "Opcional. Escribe solo tu usuario; nosotros armamos el enlace." → ES "Comparte tus redes sociales con tus clientes." / EN "Share your social media with your clients." — drops "opcional" and the technical "armamos el enlace"; the username-only instruction stays in `socialHelp`. Build green.

**Sprint 126 — left-align the "cerca de mí" filter toggle label.** The "Buscar profesionales cerca de mí" toggle (`search-filters.tsx`) rendered its label CENTERED. Root cause: it's a `<button>`, and `<button>` defaults to `text-align: center`; the label `<span>` inherited that, so when the long ES label (and EN "Find professionals near me") **wrapped to two lines** at sidebar/mobile widths, each line centered. The shorter "Solo verificados" toggle fit on one line, hiding the same latent issue. Fix: added `flex-1 text-left` to the near-me label span so the text left-aligns and fills the available width, matching the left-aligned dropdown labels (`text-left` blocks) and the rest of the filter panel. Toggle switch still pinned right via `justify-between`. Only that one span touched (per "only touch this"); the verified toggle was left as-is since it never wraps. Build green.

**Sprint 127 — footer refresh (current links) + removed dead footer.** The only footer rendered app-wide is `landing-footer.tsx` (dark, `bg-[#111827]`); the legacy light `layout/footer.tsx` was **unused dead code** that linked to non-existent `/planes` + `/nosotros` and to footer keys (`company.*`, `pros.plans`) that no longer exist — **deleted** (no imports anywhere). Updated `landing-footer.tsx` so every link points to a CURRENT page (all 12 verified to have a `page.tsx`): **Para clientes** = Buscar profesionales (/buscar), Categorías (/categorias, NEW), Cómo funciona (/como-funciona), Publicar un proyecto (/publicar-proyecto); **Para profesionales** = Registra tu perfil (/registro/profesional via `SmartRegisterLink`), Cómo atraer clientes (/atraer-clientes), Verificación de identidad (/proveedores-autorizados, NEW — matches the "Verificado" model); **Ayuda y soporte** = Centro de ayuda (/ayuda), Centro de soporte (/soporte, ticket system = primary support), Contacto (/contacto, NEW), and a **mailto soporte@contratacr.com** (NEW, the official `SUPPORT_EMAIL`). Bottom bar unchanged (Términos + Privacidad + "Hecho en Costa Rica" + © year). Link render now handles 3 kinds: `SmartRegisterLink` (register), plain `<a>` for `mailto:` (with `break-all`), internal `Link` otherwise. Tagline already accurate (creating an account is free; payment arranged directly with the pro — no "free forever" claim). i18n: added `footer.clients.categories`, `footer.pros.verification`, `footer.support.contactForm`, `footer.support.email`; `support.title` → "Ayuda y soporte" / "Help & support"; `support.contact` → "Centro de soporte" / "Support center"; ES+EN mirrored. Build green; responsive grid (`sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-5`, brand spans 2) unchanged.

**Sprint 128 — one support channel (drop redundant "Contacto").** `/contacto` ("Hablemos.") and `/soporte` ("Centro de soporte") were near-duplicates: BOTH create a ticket via `/api/contact`, and `/contacto` even linked users to `/soporte` to "open a ticket". Strategy = ONE centralized channel (the ticket system), so the redundancy was removed. **Decision: consolidate on `/soporte`.** Footer "Ayuda y soporte" column now has exactly two entries — **Centro de ayuda** (`/ayuda`, self-serve FAQ) + **Centro de soporte** (`/soporte`, the single way to contact us via tickets); removed the "Contacto" link AND the raw `mailto:` (a competing non-ticket channel). **`/contacto` now permanently redirects to `/soporte`** (server component: `getLocale()` + `redirect(\`/${locale}/soporte\`)`) so old/bookmarked/SEO links never 404 (its `contacto` i18n namespace is left unused but harmless). Repointed the only other internal link — the categorias "Contactarnos" CTA — to `/soporte`. `/soporte` is also the header-nav support link, so this is consistent app-wide. Removed unused i18n `footer.support.contactForm`/`footer.support.email` (ES+EN) and the now-dead `mailto:` render branch in `landing-footer.tsx`. **Legal contact email `soporte@contratacr.com` stays on Términos + Privacidad** (data-rights requirement) — only removed from the footer/nav. Build green.

**Sprint 143 — /buscar professional card REBUILT from scratch (design/structure only).** The card kept breaking on mobile (names cut off, bottom buttons disappearing, uneven heights) because of FIXED height + `overflow-hidden` + a two-zone identity|action layout. Rebuilt `professional-card.tsx` cleanly; **NO scheduling/availability/filtering logic touched** (`ProfessionalSchedule` reused verbatim as the action area; data flow unchanged). **Sizing:** removed `h-[452px] md:h-[244px]` + `overflow-hidden` → CONTENT-DRIVEN (`flex h-full flex-col`, no fixed height, no clipping); `h-full` makes cards stretch to equal height per row IN A GRID, while the single-column /buscar list lets each card grow to its content (one card per row → naturally even). **Structure:** ONE vertical flex column, identical section order for every card; the action area is bottom-pinned with `mt-auto` so "ver horario completo" + WhatsApp/Llamar/Solicitar are ALWAYS visible. **No clipped text:** name wraps up to 2 lines (`line-clamp-2`), service tags wrap + "+N", location wraps to a 2nd line + "+N". **Section order:** ranking number + favorite (absolute overlays; `pt-10` reserves a top band so they never overlap) → logo → company name + Verificado → personal name (first+both surnames) → price → tags → rating+reviews → location "+N" → "se desplaza" → `ProfessionalSchedule` (location selector → schedule preview + ver horario completo → action buttons). **Removed from card (profile only):** Casos de éxito, Certificaciones (and social icons — none were on the card). Whole card still clickable to the profile via a stretched low-z overlay (interactive bits `relative z-10`, favorite `z-20`). Build green. design-guide §50 rewritten as the card standard.

**Sprint 144 — /buscar card WIDTH fix (auto-fill grid) + location tabs (design/structure only).** Two follow-ups to the Sprint 143 rebuild; **no scheduling/availability/filtering logic touched.** **(1) Width — the card ballooned full-width.** After the rebuild the results list was still a single-column `flex flex-col gap-3`, so each content-driven card stretched to fill the whole ~54% results column (≈1000px on wide screens), leaving big empty space on the right (worst with few results). **Root cause:** no grid + no width constraint; a lone card filled the row. **Fix** (`buscar/page.tsx`): the list is now `grid grid-cols-[repeat(auto-fill,minmax(min(100%,300px),1fr))] gap-3` — 1 column on mobile, then as many ~300px+ columns as fit (≈3 on wide screens) beside the map. Used **`auto-fill` (NOT `auto-fit`)** on purpose: auto-fill keeps empty tracks, so a SINGLE result sits at the normal ~300px card width instead of ballooning. Equal-height-per-row preserved by flowing `h-full`: grid item (`buscar/page.tsx` wrapper) → `SaveableCard` (added `h-full` to its outer `relative` div) → `ProfessionalCard` (already `h-full`). Confirmed the arbitrary class emits real CSS (`grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(min(100%,300px),1fr))` present in the prod CSS chunk). **(2) Location selector → horizontal TABS** (`professional-schedule.tsx`), Doctoralia-style: replaced the dropdown `<select>` with a tab row — each location = `MapPin` + name; the selected tab is brand-blue with an underline (`border-b-2 border-[#009FD9] text-[#009FD9]`), the rest muted; the selected location's street **address shows directly below** the tabs (new `locAddress()` helper reads `workplaces[].address`; cov_*/videoconsulta/general have none). Row is `flex overflow-x-auto hide-scrollbar` with each tab `shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap` → SCROLLS sideways when tabs exceed the card width, NEVER wraps (so the action buttons never shift / equal-height never breaks). Single-location pros show name (+ address) directly, no tab row. Same `locationOptions`/`effectiveId`/`setSelectedLoc` state + strict per-id filter as before — UI-only. Dropped the now-unused `ChevronDown` import and the stale `md:pr-9` guard (the control sits in the bottom action area, clear of the top-right bookmark). Reused existing `schedule.{location,videoconsulta,atHome}` keys (ES+EN) — no new copy. Build green; design-guide §50 + the §169 availability-display bullet updated.

**Sprint 145 — Ubicación y cobertura persistence bug + /buscar desktop sizing + 2 copy tweaks.** Three requests. **(1) BUG: locations added under "Ubicación y cobertura" didn't persist** (`profile-editor.tsx`). **Root cause:** `handleSave` wrote `workplaces` BUNDLED with a pile of optional identity columns in ONE `update({ ...baseUpdate, ...identityFields })`; the retry fallback fired on `could not find|PGRST204|schema cache` (which ANY unmigrated optional column triggers — e.g. `contact_email` from migration 049, `coverage_provincias`/`coverage_country` from 033) and re-saved with **only `baseUpdate`**, silently dropping `workplaces` — and threw NO error, so the UI showed "Guardado" while the locations vanished (same class of bug the code already isolated for `certifications` + `social_links`). **Fix:** split the professionals write into independent updates — (1) core `baseUpdate` (throws on error); (2) **LOCATIONS in their OWN update** `{ workplaces, search_provincias, search_cantones }`, retrying with just `{ workplaces }` if `search_*` aren't migrated, and **throwing on a genuine failure** (no false "Guardado"); (3) the other optional identity columns best-effort (swallow only migration errors, re-throw the rest). Read-back was already correct (`select("*")` → `initial.workplaces` → `seedZones`). Autosave wiring (`touch()` on WorkplacesPicker/travels change) was already in place — the WRITE was the bug. **(2) /buscar desktop cards too cramped** (layout only): map column `lg:w-[46%] xl:w-[46%]` → **`lg:w-[40%] xl:w-[38%]`** (`search-results-layout.tsx`), giving the results column room; card grid min `300px` → **`340px`** (`buscar/page.tsx`) so cards are comfortable/legible (1 col mobile → 2 → ~3 on very wide screens), never cramped next to the map, never the old full-width stretch. Card internals/logic untouched. **(3a) Shortened the duplicate-time error** to "No puedes agregar la misma hora en dos ubicaciones." / "You can't add the same time in two locations." (`errLocationConflict`, ES+EN) — dropped the `{time}/{location}` second sentence; simplified the `availability-editor.tsx` call site (removed the now-unused `c`/`other` locals + args). **(3b) Autosave reassurance line:** added a muted `"Los cambios se guardan automáticamente." / "Changes are saved automatically."` (`profileEditor.autosaveNote`, ES+EN) at the bottom of EVERY autosaving profile section — implemented ONCE in the shared `Section` component (new `autosave` prop, default true) so it's identical across basic/certs/location/contact/social/lang. Build green; design-guide §49 (persist-optional-columns-separately rule + autosave-note convention) + §50 (grid min 340 / map 38–40%) updated.

**Sprint 146 — /buscar desktop back to a SINGLE vertical column (layout only).** The Sprint 144 auto-fill grid + Sprint 145 sizing tweak still rendered multi-column cards squeezed next to the map; the user wants the classic layout: filter sidebar (left) · single-column scrollable card list (middle) · sticky map (right), each card spanning the FULL width of the results column. **Change** (`buscar/page.tsx`): results container `grid grid-cols-[repeat(auto-fill,minmax(min(100%,340px),1fr))]` → **`flex flex-col gap-3`** (one card per row, full-width).
