Since it's one card per row, dropped the equal-height-per-row plumbing: removed `h-full` from the card wrapper and reverted `SaveableCard` to a plain `relative` wrapper (the article's own `h-full` is harmless in a flex column). Map width kept at `lg:w-[40%] xl:w-[38%]` (Sprint 145) so the results column has ~60% for comfortably wide cards. Card internals (location tabs, bottom-pinned actions, content-driven height), the left filter sidebar, and mobile (already single column) all untouched. Build green; design-guide §50 (Sizing + Results layout + List/map split bullets) updated to the single-column standard.

**Sprint 147 — /buscar desktop: cap the card width so it stops stretching (layout only).** The single-column cards (Sprint 146) stretched to the full results-column width and looked empty. **Fix:** cap each card at a comfortable reading width and have the column hug it, the map flex into the rest. (1) `buscar/page.tsx` card wrapper → **`w-full max-w-[500px]`** (was full-width); ≤~560px ceiling, ~500px so WhatsApp/Llamar sit side by side; `w-full` keeps mobile full-width below 500px. (2) `search-results-layout.tsx`: results column `min-w-0 flex-1` → **`min-w-0 w-full lg:w-[500px] lg:shrink-0`** (hugs the card on desktop, full width on mobile); map aside `lg:w-[40%] xl:w-[38%] lg:shrink-0` → **`lg:flex-1 lg:min-w-0`** (takes the remaining horizontal space). Single-column stacked layout, card internals, filter sidebar, and mobile all unchanged. Build green; design-guide §50 (Results layout + List/map split) updated.

**Sprint 148 — zones persistence/beforeunload root-cause + /buscar card header (price right) + max-content sizing.** Two requests. **(1) BUG (finally root-caused): "¿En qué zonas ofreces tus servicios?" still didn't save + native "Changes you made may not be saved." beforeunload kept firing.** The zones editor (WorkplacesPicker in `profile-editor.tsx`) WAS already wired to `touch()` autosave, and the Sprint 145 isolation made the locations write robust — but `handleSave` autosaves the WHOLE form in one pass, and a THROW anywhere skips the success path (`setDirty(false)`), so `dirty` stayed true → `UnsavedChangesGuard` fired the beforeunload forever. **Culprit:** the `social_links` write throws when a pro has usernames but column 056 isn't migrated in prod — aborting the save AFTER the zones had already persisted (step 2), so it looked like nothing saved and the warning stuck. **Fix:** only the CORE update and the LOCATIONS update may be fatal; identity (step 3) and social (step 5) are now best-effort — log on failure, set a SOFT warning (shown via `setError` AFTER clearing `dirty`) only for a real failure with user data present, swallow migration-missing errors. So `dirty` clears (beforeunload stops) whenever core+locations succeed, regardless of which optional column is unmigrated. Kept the global `UnsavedChangesGuard` (protects the debounce window for all sections). **(2) /buscar card internal layout** (`professional-card.tsx`, design only): moved the PRICE to the top-right of the header (right-aligned `shrink-0 max-w-[45%]` column; identity logo+name+Verificado+personal-name stays left in `min-w-0 flex-1`) so the header uses the full width and the empty top-right is gone; the price sits just below the favorite bookmark (the `pt-10` band clears it, no overlap) and is NOT `z-10` so the card overlay still routes its clicks to the profile. Personal name `line-clamp-1`→`line-clamp-2` (long names wrap, never collide with price). Tightened inter-section spacing (`mt-3`→`mt-2.5` tags, `mt-2`→`mt-1.5` location, action `pt-3`→`pt-2.5`) so a low-content card isn't airy. Max-content robustness confirmed: long company+personal names (each 2 lines), 5+ tags (wrap/+N), 5 locations (tabs scroll), busy day (chips +N) → the card just grows taller, nothing clips/collides/pushes buttons. Kept the ~500px cap, content-driven height, single-column list, location tabs, bottom-pinned actions, mobile. Build green; design-guide §49 (optional writes non-fatal rule) + §50 (header/price + max-content) updated.

**Sprint 149 — /buscar card DESKTOP two-column layout (Doctoralia style; design only).** Restructured the card so on **desktop (lg+)** it's TWO columns — professional info LEFT, schedule RIGHT — with a full-width action row spanning both; **mobile** stacks the same blocks (info, schedule, buttons). **Key architecture (server/client split):** `professional-card.tsx` is async SERVER, `professional-schedule.tsx` is the `"use client"` component holding ALL schedule state. Since the location tabs (left), schedule preview (right), and buttons (bottom) share that state, they must be ONE client instance — so **`ProfessionalSchedule` now owns the card-body LAYOUT** and the card passes its left-column info as a slot (`<ProfessionalSchedule info={…} />`). NO data/fetch/filter logic changed. **Changes:** (1) `professional-schedule.tsx`: added `info?: ReactNode` prop; unified the 3 old return branches into ONE `lg:grid lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_288px] lg:gap-5 lg:items-start` (LEFT `{info}`+locationControl; RIGHT `relative z-10` schedule+ver horario) + a full-width action row below; the schedule body now shows CONSECUTIVE day-columns from the first day with availability, with **"No disponible"** (new `schedule.dayUnavailable` ES/EN) for empty days in the window (was: packed days-with-slots only); replaced the stacked contactButtons helper with ONE wrapping `flex flex-wrap` row (WhatsApp+Llamar+Solicitar, `min-w` so it wraps on mobile, never overflows); locationControl root got `relative z-10`. (2) `professional-card.tsx`: built the `info` slot (square photo + name/Verificado/personal + price + tags + rating + location), removed per-section margins (left column uses `gap-2`), made the photo SQUARE rounded (`Avatar h-14 w-14 rounded-xl lg:h-16 lg:w-16` + rounded-xl fallback; `cn`/twMerge overrides `rounded-full`), and now renders just `<ProfessionalSchedule info=… />` + the stretched profile overlay. (3) Card width: wrapper `w-full max-w-[520px] lg:max-w-none` (`buscar/page.tsx`); results column `lg:w-[620px] xl:w-[680px] 2xl:w-[880px]` hugs the wider card, map `lg:flex-1` takes the rest (`search-results-layout.tsx`). Max-content safe (long names wrap 2 lines, 5+ tags, 5 locations tabs scroll, busy day +N → card grows). Build green; design-guide §50 rewritten as the two-column standard.

**Sprint 150 — /buscar MOBILE map-on-top (Yelp-style); desktop unchanged (design/layout only).** Restructured the mobile (<lg) /buscar shell (`search-results-layout.tsx`): (1) **Service search pinned at the very top** via a new self-contained `MobileServiceSearch` (exported from `search-filters.tsx`) that manages ONLY `q` and preserves all other params by copying the current URL. (2) **Map on TOP** (`h-[45vh]`, full width) with pins — the SAME single `GoogleMapPanel` instance, repositioned via flex `order` (NOT a 2nd map): outer `flex-col lg:flex-row`, map `order-2 lg:order-3`, results `order-3 lg:order-2`, sidebar `xl:order-1`, search `order-1 lg:hidden`; container kept `items-stretch` so the desktop sticky map still works. (3) **Results panel below:** a horizontally-scrollable **filter chips row** — new `variant="chips"` on `SearchFilters` (Categoría via CategorySearch + Provincia/Cantón/Ordenar/Aseguradora as pill `Select`s + "cerca de mí"/"Solo verificados" toggle chips; `overflow-x-auto hide-scrollbar`, never wraps), then the **"N profesionales" count** (`countLabel` prop = page subtitle), then the existing card list. **Removed** the old mobile List/Map toggle (and `List`/`MapIcon`/`cn` imports); the "Filtros" drawer button is now scoped `hidden lg:flex xl:hidden` (lg–xl only). **No filter logic changed** — chips reuse every `SearchFilters` handler (same URL params). **No clobbering:** the chips' `applyFilters` reads `q` from the URL (not the chip instance's stale local state) and the search bar copies the live URL, so the two instances don't overwrite each other's params (added a `variant` guard for the `q` base + `variant` to the `applyFilters` deps). Page-header subtitle now `hidden lg:block` (count shows in the mobile panel instead — no duplication). **Card internals UNTOUCHED** (the mobile card is the existing stacked card from Sprint 149). Build green; design-guide §51 added (responsive shell) + §50 List/map note updated.

**Sprint 151 — /buscar card action buttons CONDITIONAL on availability (logic only, no layout change).** Per the reference (Doctoralia): the bottom action row's CONTENTS now depend on whether the pro has an available schedule, but its position/layout is unchanged. In `professional-schedule.tsx`, added `hasSchedule = canBook && hasUpcoming` (the day strip is showing) and rebuilt `actionRow`: (a) `hasSchedule` → render ONLY **"Solicitar servicio"** (no WhatsApp/Llamar — the schedule + "Ver horario completo" are the path); (b) else (contact-to-coordinate state: private, WhatsApp-only, or bookable-but-no-published-slots) → render **WhatsApp** + **"Llamar" only when `showCall`** (`allow_phone_call` + a number), NO Solicitar. To use `hasUpcoming`, moved the `actionRow` definition to AFTER the day-window computation. Same full-width `relative z-10 mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5` row, same position; only WHICH buttons render changed. Reused existing `schedule.{requestService,whatsapp,call}` keys (ES+EN) — no new copy. Matches the attached reference (Melissa = no public availability → contact note + WhatsApp + Llamar; Guiselle = schedule strip → Solicitar). Build green; design-guide §50 action-row bullet updated.

**Sprint 152 — /buscar card matches HuliHealth (design/layout only; conditional logic unchanged).** (1) **Action buttons moved OUT of the full-width bottom strip INTO the right column** (`professional-schedule.tsx`); removed the bottom strip entirely. Replaced the single `actionRow` const with reusable pieces — `verHorarioButton` (now an OUTLINED full-width button `border border-[#009FD9] text-[#009FD9] hover:bg-[#EBF5FB]`, was a tiny text link), `solicitarButton` (filled `bg-[#009FD9]` full-width), `contactButtons` (WhatsApp + Llamar STACKED, each `w-full`). Right column now renders: schedule-or-message ON TOP, then `hasSchedule ? (verHorario + Solicitar) : contactButtons`. Same conditional as Sprint 151 (which buttons appear is unchanged). On mobile the right column stacks below the left → info → schedule/message → buttons; the outlined Ver horario style applies there too. (2) **Price → info icon at the top of the left column** (`professional-card.tsx`): wrapped the price in `shrink-0 flex max-w-[45%] items-start gap-1` with a small grey `Info` icon (lucide) after it (HuliHealth style), on the name line, right-aligned. (3) Kept all CR-specific elements (ranking badge, Verificado, "se desplaza", location tabs, favorite bookmark) and the square photo unchanged. Reused existing i18n (`viewFullSchedule`/`requestService`/`whatsapp`/`call`) — no new copy; info icon is decorative (`aria-hidden`). Build green; design-guide §50 updated (buttons in right column, outlined Ver horario, price+info-icon).

**Sprint 153 — /buscar card: fix the two-column PROPORTIONS to ~65/35 (HuliHealth).** The Sprint 149/152 grid used a FIXED `288px` right column (`lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_288px]`), which on the common ~620px card was ~49% — near-equal columns, not HuliHealth's ~⅓. Verified the grid CSS DID generate (so the two-column structure was working; the "full-width strip / info below" the user saw was a stale pre-two-column deploy). **Fix (one line, desktop layout only):** `lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1.85fr)_minmax(0,1fr)]` → ~65% left / ~35% right, FRACTIONAL so it scales (right stays ~⅓ at lg/xl/2xl instead of ballooning to ~half on narrow cards); `minmax(0,…)` keeps both columns shrinkable so long content never overflows. Structure unchanged (already correct since Sprint 152): LEFT = all info + location tabs/address; RIGHT = schedule-or-message + its buttons (outlined Ver horario, filled Solicitar, or WhatsApp/Llamar); nothing spans both columns; overlays (ranking badge, favorite) only. Mobile stacked single-column unchanged. No logic / button-conditional change. Build green (CSS emits `grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1.85fr) minmax(0,1fr)`); design-guide §50 desktop-layout bullet updated.

**Sprint 154 — /buscar MOBILE: clean top-to-bottom flow, no overlap, chips ABOVE the map (mobile layout only).** The mobile results were overlapping the map. Rebuilt the mobile flow in `search-results-layout.tsx` as NORMAL DOM ORDER (no mobile `order`/sticky/absolute/negative-margins): search → filter chips → map → count → list. Changes: (1) the filter chips row moved OUT of the results panel to its own `lg:hidden` block ABOVE the map (was below it). (2) DOM order is now search, chips, sidebar(hidden<xl), map, results — so on mobile (`flex flex-col`, no order applied) it reads top-to-bottom naturally; desktop (`lg:flex-row`) uses `lg:order-1/2/3` (sidebar · results · map) — unchanged. (3) Map mobile height `h-[45vh]`→`h-[40vh]` and added `relative` to its box (with `overflow-hidden`) so the Google Maps canvas can never escape/overlap; `lg:sticky lg:top-20` kept for desktop only. Desktop layout, card internals, and all logic untouched. Build green.

**Sprint 155 — REVERT /buscar card DESKTOP to single-column (keep responsive/mobile updates).** User didn't like the desktop TWO-COLUMN card (Sprints 149-153) and asked to revert ONLY desktop, keeping the mobile/responsive card exactly. Investigated: the two-column was purely ADDITIVE `lg:` classes on the mobile single-column base, so it IS cleanly separable (confirmed via `git diff` — only `lg:`/`xl:`/`2xl:` classes changed; mobile base byte-identical). Asked the user (AskUserQuestion) → answer "revert desktop keep responsive updates" → Option 1. **Changes (desktop-only):** (1) `professional-schedule.tsx`: wrapper `flex flex-col gap-3 lg:grid lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1.85fr)_minmax(0,1fr)] lg:gap-5 lg:items-start` → **`flex flex-col gap-3`** (single column at all widths; desktop now renders the same single column mobile already used). (2) `search-results-layout.tsx`: desktop results column `lg:w-[620px] xl:w-[680px] 2xl:w-[880px]` → **`lg:w-[500px]`** so the single-column card isn't oversized; map `lg:flex-1` takes the rest. Card wrapper (`buscar/page.tsx`) NOT touched (mobile `max-w-[520px]` intact; `lg:max-w-none` fills the 500px column). **Mobile is byte-identical** (all changes `lg:`-scoped; the removed grid classes never applied on mobile). The desktop card keeps ALL responsive updates (square photo, location tabs, conditional buttons, outlined Ver horario, price+info icon, day "No disponible") — just stacked in one column instead of two. Build green; design-guide §50 rewritten (single-column standard; how to re-add a desktop two-column ADDITIVELY if ever wanted).

**Sprint 156 — /buscar card DESKTOP redesigned COMPACT HORIZONTAL (mobile untouched).** User now wants the desktop card compact/horizontal so MORE cards fit per screen (info LEFT, schedule+buttons RIGHT, shorter cards). Re-introduced a desktop two-column — but ADDITIVELY (`lg:` only) on the mobile single-column base, and tuned compact. **Changes (all `lg:`-scoped → mobile byte-identical, confirmed via git diff):** (1) `professional-schedule.tsx` wrapper `flex flex-col gap-3` → **`flex flex-col gap-3 lg:grid lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_260px] lg:gap-4 lg:items-start`** (info+tabs LEFT/larger, schedule+buttons in a fixed 260px RIGHT rail; tighter desktop col gaps `lg:gap-1.5`). (2) `professional-card.tsx`: name h3 + personal-name p `line-clamp-2` → `line-clamp-2 lg:line-clamp-1` (desktop names 1 line = shorter card; mobile keeps 2). (3) `search-results-layout.tsx` desktop results column `lg:w-[500px]` → **`lg:w-[640px] xl:w-[700px] 2xl:w-[820px]`** to fit the two-column card; map `lg:flex-1`. Card wrapper (`buscar/page.tsx`) untouched (mobile `max-w-[520px]` intact; `lg:max-w-none` fills the wider column). ALL card info/features kept (name/company, Verificado, profession tags, location +N, "se desplaza", reviews, price+info-icon, location tabs + per-location schedules, day strip with "No disponible", conditional buttons, stretched profile overlay) — just arranged horizontally so each card is ~half the height. CSS confirmed `grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr) 260px`. **Mobile is byte-identical** (every change additive `lg:`). Build green; design-guide §50 rewritten (mobile-base + additive-desktop GOLDEN RULE; compact two-column).

**Sprint 157 — /buscar card: group the location selector WITH its schedule slots (fix the gap).** The location label/selector looked disconnected from its slots: `locationControl` lived in the LEFT column (with info) while `scheduleBody` (slots) was in the RIGHT column — so on desktop they were in DIFFERENT columns and on mobile split by the outer `gap-3`. **Fix (`professional-schedule.tsx`):** moved `locationControl` OUT of the left column INTO the right rail, directly above the slots, tightly grouped: `<div className="flex flex-col gap-1">{locationControl}{scheduleBody}</div>`. Now LEFT = info only; RIGHT = location selector + its schedule (one `gap-1` group) + buttons. So each location label/tabs + its time slots read as ONE connected unit on both desktop (same rail) and mobile (`gap-1` instead of the old `gap-3` between blocks). The info's own location LINE ("San Ramón, Alajuela" + "se desplaza") stays in the info (general summary). No logic change (same `effectiveId`/`setSelectedLoc`/filtering); applies to mobile + desktop (improves grouping, doesn't break mobile). Build green; design-guide §50 updated.

**Sprint 158 — /buscar MOBILE redesigned to map-background + DRAGGABLE BOTTOM SHEET (desktop untouched).** User wants the modern map-search pattern (Yelp / Apple-Maps / Airbnb / Uber): on mobile the MAP fills the screen as the background and the professional cards live in a draggable sheet OVER the map — swipe the sheet up for more cards, down for more map — so map + results are seen together. Replaced the old stacked "map-on-top, list-below" mobile flow. **All in `search-results-layout.tsx` (`"use client"`); every mobile class carries a `lg:` reset so DESKTOP renders byte-identically** (verified: desktop columns/widths/sticky map unchanged; only extra desktop prop is a harmless `position:relative` on the shell, no positioned descendant on desktop). **Structure:** (1) the shell becomes a one-viewport-tall full-bleed region — `relative -mx-4 -mt-4 h-[calc(100dvh-8rem)] overflow-hidden sm:-mx-6` (resets `lg:mx-0 lg:mt-0 lg:h-auto lg:overflow-visible lg:flex lg:flex-row lg:gap-5`). (2) the SINGLE `<GoogleMapPanel>` map = `absolute inset-0 z-0` full-bleed background (inner box `h-full w-full`, no border/radius on mobile; resets `lg:static lg:order-3 lg:flex-1` + the sticky `lg:h-[calc(100vh-104px)] lg:rounded-2xl lg:border lg:sticky lg:top-20`). (3) the results = a BOTTOM SHEET `absolute inset-x-0 bottom-0 z-30 flex flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-t-2xl border bg-white shadow-[0_-8px_24px_rgba(0,0,0,.12)]` (resets `lg:static lg:order-2 lg:w-[640px] xl:w-[700px] 2xl:w-[820px] lg:h-auto lg:bg-transparent lg:border-0 lg:shadow-none lg:rounded-none lg:overflow-visible`). Inside the sheet, top→bottom: a **grab handle** (`lg:hidden`, the ONLY drag target so the card list still scrolls natively), the **search + filter chips + count** header (`lg:hidden`: `MobileServiceSearch` + `<SearchFilters variant="chips"/>` + "N profesionales"), then the **card list** in a `flex-1 overflow-y-auto overscroll-contain` scroll area (`lg:flex-none lg:overflow-visible` desktop). **Drag = HEIGHT-based (NOT transform):** sheet height = `100 - sheetPct`% of the region via inline `style.height` applied ONLY when `isMobile` (so it never leaks past `lg:`). Height-sizing keeps the inner scroll area equal to the visible window — a `translateY` approach leaves the sheet box taller than the window so the LAST cards fall below the fold, unreachable (caught + fixed during this sprint). Detents `SNAP = { full:12, mid:52, peek:80 }` (% map at top; default mid); pointer handlers on the handle update `sheetPct`, release snaps to nearest, a <6px tap toggles full↔mid. SSR default `h-[48%]` (=mid) prevents a pre-hydration flash; `isMobile` from `matchMedia("(max-width:1023px)")`. ONE map instance only (never a hidden 2nd `<GoogleMapPanel>`). No filtering/search LOGIC change; the page (`buscar/page.tsx`) was NOT touched — it already passes `countLabel`/`mobileSearch`/`mobileFilters`. Build green; design-guide §51 rewritten.

**Sprint 159 — /buscar card: apply the HuliHealth design-handoff details (approved over the old guide).** A design handoff (`design_handoff_buscar/README.md` + `buscar-prototype.html`) specced a /buscar redesign. Most of its STRUCTURE was already live (compact horizontal card, 3-column desktop, mobile bottom sheet, location tabs, schedule strip, conditional buttons, numbered-pin↔card sync, bookmark, price+rating layout, real Google Maps). Asked the user about the parts that CONFLICTED with our canonical design guide: (1) brand blue #008ce0 vs our #009FD9 → **KEEP #009FD9**; (2) Poppins vs Inter → **KEEP Inter** (added a `TODO(brand)` in `globals.css` where `--font-sans` is set — possible future migration, NOT this ticket); (3) card details → **APPLY the handoff** (now the approved rule for /buscar, design guide updated, not an exception). **Changes:** (a) `professional-card.tsx`: "Verificado" is now **GREEN TEXT** (`text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#16a34a]`, no icon, no pill) on its OWN line between the company name and personal name (was a solid blue `Badge variant="verified"` pill inline on the name line); removed the now-unused `Badge` import. The blue Verificado pill stays canonical on profile/dashboard/saved-pros mockup — green text is the search card ONLY. (b) `professional-schedule.tsx`: the bookable case now shows a **SINGLE FILLED "Ver horario completo"** pill (`rounded-full bg-[#009FD9] text-white py-2.5`) — removed the second "Solicitar servicio" button (booking happens inside Ver horario). The contact case now has **both buttons FILLED** pills: WhatsApp `rounded-full bg-[#25D366]`, "Llamar" `rounded-full bg-[#009FD9]` (was outlined). All three actions are `rounded-full py-2.5 w-full`. No schedule/booking LOGIC changed. Design guide §50 updated (buttons bullet + a new Verificado-color bullet); brand blue + Inter unchanged. Build green.

**Sprint 160 — FIX the recurring "Ubicación y cobertura no guarda" bug (workplaces never persisted on a single add).** The pro profile editor (`profile-editor.tsx`) lets a pro add work zones (`WorkplacesPicker` → `Lugares agregados (N)`). Reported repeatedly across sessions: add a place → UI shows "Lugares agregados (1)" → reload → gone. Prior fixes targeted the WRITE path (made `workplaces`/`search_*` a separate FATAL `.update()` with a missing-column retry) — but the write was never wrong; the **DATA it wrote was stale**. **ROOT CAUSE = stale closure in the debounced autosave.** `touch()` (called from `onChange={(next)=>{ setWorkplaces(next); touch(); }}`) ran `autoTimer.current = setTimeout(() => { void handleSave(true); }, 1000)`. `touch` executes SYNCHRONOUSLY inside the onChange, BEFORE React re-renders, so the `handleSave` captured in that closure sees PRE-change state. For a discrete single edit (`workplaces` [] → [A]) the 1s timer fired the stale `handleSave`, which read `workplaces = []`, wrote `[]`, "succeeded", and set `dirty=false`. The UI showed [A] (state did update) but the DB got [] — and because `dirty` was cleared, the unmount-flush (which DOES use the up-to-date `saveRef.current`) saw nothing pending, so the real value [A] was orphaned in React state only. This also silently dropped the LAST keystroke of any text field if the user then sat idle (same staleness, just less visible). **FIX (one line, `profile-editor.tsx` `touch()`):** the deferred autosave now calls `saveRef.current?.(true)` instead of `handleSave(true)`. `saveRef.current = handleSave` is reassigned on EVERY render (line 213), so by the time the 1s timer fires (well after the re-render that applied the new state) the ref points at a `handleSave` closing over the CURRENT `workplaces` ([A]). No schema/RLS/write-bundling change was needed — `workplaces` (migration 022) and `search_provincias`/`search_cantones` (migration 030) already exist; the read path (`select("*")` + `pro.workplaces ?? []`) was already correct. Build green.

**Sprint 161 — /buscar card: match the HuliHealth target screenshots (location moves to the LEFT column, circular photo + navy rank badge, vertical divider).** Pixel-pass over the result card to match the attached target screenshots, staying in-stack (no new primitives) and keeping brand `#009FD9` + Inter. **Changes:** (1) **Avatar is now CIRCULAR** — `professional-card.tsx` `<Avatar className="h-14 w-14 rounded-full lg:h-16 lg:w-16">` (+ `rounded-full` on the fallback), was `rounded-xl` (square). **This supersedes §50's "SQUARE photo".** (2) **Numbered rank badge now RIDES ON THE AVATAR** (navy `#162543`, `absolute -top-1.5 -left-1.5 h-[22px] w-[22px] ring-2 ring-white`) instead of the page-wrapper top-left — added a `rank?: number` prop to `ProfessionalCard`, passed `rank={i + 1}` from `buscar/page.tsx`, and removed the wrapper's blue `<span>` badge. Map pins still use `numbering` (unchanged). (3) **Location tabs + address MOVED from the RIGHT rail INTO the LEFT column, under the rating** (`professional-schedule.tsx`): LEFT = `{info}{locationControl}`, RIGHT = `{scheduleBody}` + button. **This supersedes Sprint 157** (which had grouped the location WITH the schedule in the right rail). The redundant left-column location SUMMARY line (`fixedText`/`mobileText` with MapPin/Truck) was removed from the card's `info` (the tabs replace it); `prettyPlace` + the `MapPin`/`Truck` imports dropped from the card. (4) **`locationControl` rebuilt as a tab row on a hairline divider, then the address** — and **always shown** (decoupled from `slots.length`; was gated on having published slots) so a pro with NO public schedule (e.g. PintaPro, Luis Mora) still shows WHERE they work. When the pro has no named workplaces it falls back to a single static **province/cantón tab** + the coverage/`cantón, provincia` address. New props on `ProfessionalSchedule`: `placeFallback`, `placeAddress`, `coverageText`, `businessName` (the last bolds the venue prefix on a real workplace address; passed explicitly because the schedule types `professional` as the mock type which lacks `businessName`). (5) **Vertical divider between the columns** — the right rail now has `border-t pt-3` (mobile) → `lg:border-l lg:pl-4 lg:justify-center` (desktop), and the grid dropped `lg:items-start` so the columns stretch to equal height (full-height divider; schedule centers against the taller left column). Right rail widened `260px → 300px`, gap `lg:gap-4 → lg:gap-5`. (6) **Removed the `pt-10` top band** on the card article (the rank no longer needs it); the header gets `pr-8 lg:pr-0` so the price clears the top-right bookmark on mobile (on desktop the price sits in the left column, far from it). Schedule/booking LOGIC, slot pill colors, and the conditional-button rule (Sprint 159) are unchanged. Build green; design-guide §50 updated.

**Sprint 162 — /buscar card + map: schedule-grid and map-zoom polish to match the target screenshots.** Follow-up on Sprint 161, fixing the four concrete gaps the user called out against the attached design screenshots. **Changes:** (1) **Right rail narrowed `300px → 292px`** and the body grid gained a **`lg:min-h-[184px]` floor** (`professional-schedule.tsx` `lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_292px] lg:gap-5 lg:min-h-[184px]`). Combined with the existing `lg:justify-center` on the right rail, this removes the "large empty gap above *Ver horario completo*" — the schedule + button now center within a consistent card height that aligns evenly with the left content. (2) **Day columns now use RELATIVE labels** — new `dayColumnLabel(d, i, locale)` (replaces `dateLabel`): index 0 → "Hoy"/"Today", index 1 → "Mañana"/"Tomorrow", else capitalized short weekday + day number ("Jue 18"/"Thu 18"). **This deliberately reverses Sprint ~156's always-full "18 jun" label** because the user's target screenshots show Hoy/Mañana/"Jue 18". Label styling muted to `text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#6b7280]` (normal case — dropped the `uppercase`/`font-bold`/blue-for-soon treatment). (3) **Each day is its own equal-width grid cell** (already `grid-cols-3`, label on top + pills stacked), now showing **up to 3 time pills** (was 2) before the dashed `+N`, with roomier pills (`py-0.5 → py-1`) and spacing (`gap-1 → gap-1.5` within a column, `gap-1.5 → gap-2` between columns) so the strip is no longer cramped. (4) **Map (`google-map-panel.tsx`) `fitToMarkers`** now caps zoom at **maxZoom 12** (was 15) with padding **64** (was 48) so the pin cluster stays focused on the result area instead of showing the whole country; single-pin zoom `14 → 13`. (5) Confirmed (no change) the **initials avatar** (light-blue `#EBF5FB` circle + `#009FD9` initials) is the no-photo fallback — `avatarUrl` resolves to `null` (`row.profiles?.avatar_url ?? null`), never a logo placeholder. NOTE: the live `/buscar` map is **Google Maps** (`GoogleMapPanel`), not Leaflet — the "Leaflet | CARTO" attribution in the user's screenshots is from the `buscar-prototype.html` design reference. Build green; design-guide §50 UPDATE (Sprint 162) added.

**Sprint 163 — /buscar card + map: six target-screenshot mismatches (pricing, map points, reseñas, profesiones, favorites icon) + exact contact copy.** User pass over the live card vs. the attached target screenshots. **Changes:** (1) **Pricing** (`professional-card.tsx`) — the price now renders the AMOUNT in brand-blue `#009FD9` and the `/unit` in muted grey `#9ca3af` (split on the first `/`: `priceAmount`/`priceUnit`/`priceIsColones`), and the **`Info` icon was removed** (import dropped). The gate widened from `priceLabel.includes("₡")` to `priceLabel` so a text price ("Precio a consultar") also shows, in grey. **Supersedes Sprint 161's black-price + Info-icon treatment.** (2) **Map points** (`google-map-panel.tsx`) — teardrop pins and cluster bubbles recolored brand-blue `#009FD9 → navy #162543` so they MATCH the navy rank badge riding on each card's avatar (pin #N ↔ card #N, same color). (3) **Reseñas** (`professional-card.tsx`) — removed the `·` dot between rating and count; the count is now wrapped in parentheses and muted grey (`text-[#9ca3af]`, was brand-blue) → "★ 4.8 (47 reseñas)". (4) **Profesiones** — profession chips cap raised `slice(0,2) → slice(0,3)` so a 3-profession pro (e.g. Nutrición Integral CR: Nutrición / Nutrición clínica / Nutrición deportiva) shows all three before the `+N`. (5) **Favorites icon** (`save-button.tsx`) — dropped the circular white bordered button; now a PLAIN bookmark outline (`h-[18px]`, grey `#9ca3af`, no border/bg), filled brand-blue when saved. (6) **Exact contact copy** (`messages/es.json` + `en.json`, `schedule` namespace — consumed ONLY by `professional-schedule.tsx`): `availabilityHiddenNote` AND `noScheduleNote` → "La disponibilidad de este profesional no es pública. Contáctanos y conoce sus horarios."; `whatsapp` "WhatsApp → Contáctanos por WhatsApp"; `call` "Llamar → Contáctanos por llamada" (EN parity updated too). NOTE confirmed: the live `/buscar` map is **Google Maps** (`GoogleMapPanel`), not Leaflet — the screenshots' "Leaflet | CARTO" credit is the prototype's. Initials avatar (light-blue `#EBF5FB` + `#009FD9`) already correct (no logo placeholder). Build green; design-guide §50 UPDATE (Sprint 163) added.

**Sprint 164 — /buscar: coral availability note (icon + colour), blue "Precio a consultar", redesigned results header.** Three follow-ups from the attachment. (1) **Coral availability note** (`professional-schedule.tsx` `scheduleNote`) — the "La disponibilidad de este profesional no es pública…" panel was grey-on-grey with NO icon; now it's a **`CalendarDays` icon + text in coral `#DC5B4B` on a pale coral panel** (`bg-[#FDF3F1] border-[#F7D8D1]`, `flex items-start gap-2`). This coral is a deliberate single-purpose accent for the "availability not public" state; the general "serious app, avoid colorful icons" rule (memory [[serious-restrained-icon-tone]]) still holds elsewhere — called out in a code comment. (2) **"Precio a consultar" now matches the price colour** (`professional-card.tsx`) — text prices were grey `#6b7280`; the amount span is now ALWAYS brand-blue `#009FD9` (colones at `text-[15px]`, the longer text price at `text-[13px]` so it fits). (3) **Results header redesigned** (`buscar/page.tsx` "Todos los profesionales / N profesionales en Costa Rica") — was a flat white strip; now a **subtle brand-tinted panel** `bg-[#f4f9fc]` with a **brand accent bar** (`h-6 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#009FD9]`) to the left of the title, title+subtitle grouped beside it, `py-3 → py-4`. Mobile still hides the subtitle (shown in the results panel instead). Build green; design-guide §50 UPDATE (Sprint 164) added.

**Sprint 165 — /buscar card: favorites icon no longer touches the note; per-location "no times" message.** Two fixes. (1) **Bookmark vs. note overlap** (`professional-schedule.tsx`) — on desktop the right rail reached the card's right edge, so the top-right favorites bookmark (`SaveableCard`, `absolute top-2.5 right-2.5`, ~26px box) overlapped the coral availability note's top-right corner. Added **`lg:pr-6`** to the right rail so all right-column content (note AND the day strip) ends ~40px from the card edge, leaving the bookmark a clear top-right gutter. Mobile unaffected (bookmark sits over the header there, which already has `pr-8`). (2) **Two distinct no-schedule notes by case** — previously every empty state used the same "…no es pública…" text even when the pro DOES publish hours, just not at the selected location. Added `hasUpcomingAnywhere` (a `useMemo` over the unfiltered `slots`, same WINDOW_DAYS/lead-time rules as `days`). Now: `!canBook` (private / WhatsApp-only) → `availabilityHiddenNote` ("…no es pública…"); `canBook && !hasUpcoming` → if `hasUpcomingAnywhere && locTabs.length > 1` (the pro books at ANOTHER location) show the NEW **`noTimesAtLocation`** = "No hay horarios disponibles en esta ubicación. Elige otra ubicación o contáctanos para coordinar." (EN: "No available times at this location…"), else fall back to `availabilityHiddenNote`. Note buttons unchanged (contact buttons). `noScheduleNote` key is now unused (kept for safety). Build green; design-guide §50 UPDATE (Sprint 165) added.

**Sprint 166 — /buscar card: a SINGLE location now renders in the active/selected color.** Bug: a pro with exactly ONE named workplace showed that location tab GREY (inactive), unlike a selected tab in the multi-location case (brand-blue `#009FD9` + underline). ROOT CAUSE in `professional-schedule.tsx`: `defaultLoc` was gated on `hasLocationSelector = locationOptions.length > 1`, so for a single location `defaultLoc` was `null` → `effectiveId = null` → the tab's `active = (o.id === effectiveId)` was `false`. FIX: `defaultLoc` now resolves whenever `locationOptions.length > 0` (first location with slots, else `locationOptions[0].id`), so `effectiveId` points at the single location → its tab is `active` (brand-blue + underline), matching a selected multi-location tab. Side benefit: a single-location pro now also shows that workplace's street address (via `workplaceAddr = locAddress(effectiveId)`, business name bolded) instead of falling back to "cantón, provincia"; `filteredSlots` is unaffected (a single location's slots all match, general slots still included). Removed the now-unused `hasLocationSelector`. The province/cantón FALLBACK tab (no named workplaces) was already always-active. Build green; design-guide §50 UPDATE (Sprint 166) added.

**Sprint 167 — /buscar card: relocate the favorites bookmark so it stops reserving an ugly blank side gutter.** Sprint 165 cleared the bookmark↔note overlap with `lg:pr-6` on the right rail, but that shifted ALL right-column content left and left a visible blank strip beside the full-width buttons. Replaced it: the right rail now uses **`lg:pt-6` (a small TOP band) instead of `lg:pr-6`**, so the bookmark sits in the top-right CORNER above the content and the schedule/note/buttons stay FULL-WIDTH (no horizontal blank space). With `lg:justify-center`, the content centers in the space below the reserved top band — guaranteeing the bookmark (top-2.5, ~26px tall) clears the content in every case. Mobile unaffected (bookmark is over the header there, `pr-8`). Build green; design-guide §50 UPDATE (Sprint 167) added.

**Sprint 168 — traveling pros can OPTIONALLY publish "A domicilio" availability (reusing the cov_* schedule mechanism).** A pro who selected "Me desplazo donde el cliente" (`service_type` includes `"mobile"`) previously could ONLY coordinate via WhatsApp — they had no way to publish bookable travel hours, because the profile editor stores travel as a simple boolean and ALWAYS saves `coverage_areas: []` (so the availability editor's existing `cov_*` coverage-zone options never appeared). FIX, reusing the per-location schedule structure end-to-end (NO parallel system): (1) `availability-editor.tsx` — new `travels?: boolean` prop; when true, `locationOptions` gains ONE extra option `{ id: "cov_domicilio", label: t("aDomicilio")="A domicilio" }` (constant `A_DOMICILIO_LOC = "cov_domicilio"`, starts with `cov_` so it slots into the existing coverage-zone path), listed after any physical workplaces. A small hint (`aDomicilioHint`) shows under the location dropdown when "A domicilio" is selected, clarifying it's optional (no hours → WhatsApp coordination). (2) `dashboard/profesional/page.tsx` — passes `travels={String(pro.service_type ?? "").includes("mobile")}` to the editor. (3) `/buscar` needed NO changes: the schedule already maps `cov_*` → `locLabel`="A domicilio" with no street address, `locationOptions` already unions slot locationIds, and the buscar query already selects `location_id`. So cov_domicilio slots automatically appear as a selectable **"A domicilio"** tab with its own per-location slots + "Ver horario completo"; a traveling pro who adds NONE keeps the existing no-schedule WhatsApp behavior (OPTIONAL, never forced). Booking modal shows "Lugar: A domicilio" (it just passes the label through — no workplace lookup). Updated `needLocation` copy (ES/EN) to drop the removed **videoconsulta** reference and point pros to "Me desplazo donde el cliente" instead; videoconsulta stays OUT. Build green. design-guide §52 (availability editor) updated.

**Sprint 169 — /buscar polish batch 1: Verificado color, results-header bg/shadow.** (1) **Verificado consistency** — the /buscar card's "Verificado" was GREEN TEXT `#16a34a`; now it's a compact **brand-blue pill `bg-[#009FD9] text-white`**, the SAME color as the canonical `Badge variant="verified"` used in the professional dashboard/panel (cross-surface consistency, per user). (2+3) **Results header** (`buscar/page.tsx`) — background changed `#f4f9fc → #f4f7fa` so it MATCHES the results/cards area exactly, the **`border-b` divider line was removed**, and a **subtle soft shadow** (`shadow-[0_4px_10px_-6px_rgba(0,0,0,0.12)]` + `relative z-10`) now separates the header from the content cleanly (no hard line). The brand accent bar + title/subtitle layout are unchanged. Build green; design-guide §50/§51 updated.

**Sprint 170 — /buscar card: ADAPTIVE schedule day-columns + location-tab navigation for >3 places.** (1) **Adaptive days** (`professional-schedule.tsx`) — the day strip showed COLS=3 CONSECUTIVE calendar days from the first available one, rendering "No disponible" filler for gap days. Now it shows ONLY days that actually have availability (`availableDays = days.filter(d => d.items.length > 0)`) and sizes the grid to how many are shown: `dayColsClass` = `grid-cols-1` (1 day → full-width, largest pills) / `grid-cols-2` (2 days → halves) / `grid-cols-3` (3+). Paging (`offset`, ‹ ›) now walks `availableDays` COLS at a time. Result: no empty "No disponible" columns; few-day pros fill the space. Labels stay correct (Hoy/Mañana/"Jue 18") since each is computed per real date even when the available days are non-consecutive. (2) **Location-tab nav** — when a pro has MORE THAN 3 location tabs, chevron buttons now flank the scrollable tab row (`showLocNav = locTabs.length > 3`; `locScrollRef.scrollBy(±140)`) so all locations are reachable without breaking the card (row still scrolls by touch/trackpad). New aria keys `prevLocations`/`nextLocations` (ES/EN). Build green; design-guide §50 updated.

**Sprint 171 — /buscar filters (sidebar) redesign — cleaner, more modern.** `search-filters.tsx` sidebar variant: (1) added a **"Filtros" header** with a live active-count and an inline "Limpiar (N)" clear (the clear moved OUT of the bottom into the header — no duplication). (2) **Provincia + Cantón now share a 2-column row** (`grid grid-cols-2`) instead of stacking — matches the target screenshot and cuts vertical sprawl. (3) **Toggle rows ("Cerca de mí", "Solo verificados") are now plain rows** (label left + switch right, whole row a subtle `hover:bg-[#f9fafb]`), NOT bordered boxes; switches enlarged `h-4 w-7 → h-5 w-9`. (4) Search input is a soft **filled** field (`bg-[#f9fafb]` → white on focus); panel padding `p-3 → p-4`, field gap `2.5 → 3`; labels unified (`fieldLabel`, `text-[11px] font-semibold`). All filter LOGIC/URL handling unchanged (mobile chips variant untouched). Build green; design-guide §51 updated.

**Sprint 172 — /buscar map: clean light (CARTO-like) basemap + numbered navy pins.** `google-map-panel.tsx`. To match the example screenshots' light/low-clutter basemap, the map now applies an inline **`LIGHT_STYLE`** (muted grey land `#f3f4f6`, white roads, light-blue water `#d6eaf2`, POI/transit/road-label clutter hidden, only locality labels kept). Google's **vector `mapId` IGNORES inline `styles`**, so this required dropping `mapId` (removed the `MAP_ID` import) and switching from `AdvancedMarkerElement` → **legacy `google.maps.Marker`**: navy `#162543` teardrop SVG icon (`pinIcon`) with the card NUMBER drawn as the marker `label` (white, in the head via `labelOrigin`), so pins still match the numbered cards (`numbering[pro.id] = i+1` ↔ card `rank={i+1}`); cluster bubbles are navy circles (`clusterIcon`) with the count label. Hover/click moved from DOM listeners to `marker.addListener("mouseover"/"mouseout"/"click", …)`; InfoWindow preview (avatar + name + ★ + "Ver perfil") and the pin↔card highlight/scroll sync are unchanged. `clickableIcons:false` reduces POI distraction; fit-bounds (maxZoom 12, padding 64) unchanged. NOTE: `google.maps.Marker` is deprecated-but-functional (no removal date); the alternative — keeping AdvancedMarkers AND a light style — would need a CLOUD-styled Map ID (`NEXT_PUBLIC_GOOGLE_MAPS_MAP_ID`) which only the account owner can configure. Build green; needs a live visual check at contratacr.com. design-guide §(map) note added.

**Sprint 173 — profile completion: end the "100% complete + te falta Verificado" contradiction.** `profile-completion.tsx`. Verification (cédula) is OPTIONAL and never counts toward % — but at 100% the card showed `completeSubtitle` = "Excelente. Solo te falta la insignia de Verificado." (te falta ⇒ reads as missing) AND a verification block, i.e. a contradiction + duplication. Fixes: (1) **`completeSubtitle` reworded** to positive, verification-free copy — ES "Tu perfil ya está listo para recibir clientes." / EN "Your profile is ready to receive clients." So "100% / completo" no longer says anything is missing; verification is mentioned ONCE (the block). (2) **Verification block reframed as OPTIONAL opportunity** — badge changed `Recomendado → "Opcional"` (brand-blue `#EBF5FB`/`#009FD9` instead of grey), benefit now "Opcional, pero la insignia… te elijan.", a "Verificar" action link (sm+). (3) **DISMISSIBLE** — added an "×" (`dismissVerify` = "Ahora no"/"Not now") that hides the invite and PERSISTS in `localStorage` (`contratacr_verify_dismissed_<proId>`), so it never reappears; the pro can still verify from the Verificación tab. The early-return became `if (complete && !showVerify) return null` (`showVerify = !verified && !dismissedVerify`), so a complete profile with verification done OR dismissed shows nothing. New keys `optional`/`verifyAction`/`dismissVerify` (ES/EN); `recommended` now unused (kept). Build green. design-guide §(profile completion) note added.

**Sprint 174 — /buscar filters: Provincia + Cantón back to FULL-WIDTH (consistent with the other filters).** Sprint 171 had put Provincia + Cantón in a `grid grid-cols-2` row. In the 256px (`w-64`) sidebar each half-width `SelectTrigger` was ~107px — too narrow for "Todas las provincias"/"Todos los cantones" (the value overflowed/cramped), and the **disabled Cantón's faded border** (`disabled:opacity-50`) sat directly beside Provincia, so the pair looked visually off vs the full-width Categoría/Ordenar/Aseguradora. FIX (`search-filters.tsx`, styling only — filtering unchanged): unwrapped the 2-col grid so **Provincia and Cantón are full-width stacked**, direct children of the `flex flex-col gap-3` field stack, each identical to the other filters (same `SelectTrigger` `h-10 w-full rounded-xl border-[#e5e7eb]`, same `FILTER_TRIGGER` focus override, same `fieldLabel`). This **supersedes the Sprint 171 "Provincia + Cantón share a 2-column row"** note. Build green; design-guide §51 updated.

**Sprint 175 — /buscar map: refined basemap + hover/tap MINI-CARD preview (with price).** `google-map-panel.tsx` + `buscar/page.tsx`. (1) **Basemap polish** — refined `LIGHT_STYLE` to a cleaner Positron-like palette (land `#f6f7f9`, natural `#eef1f4`, white roads with subtle `#e7eaee` strokes, water `#cfe3ee`, POI/transit/neighborhood labels hidden). (2) **Mini-card preview reworked** — the InfoWindow now shows **photo · name · profession · ★rating(count) · price** (brand-blue). Price added end-to-end: `buscar/page.tsx` computes `primaryPricingLabel(pro.pricing, pro.hourlyRate)` into `mapData.priceLabel`, new `MapProfessional.priceLabel`. **Behavior split by pointer:** DESKTOP (`matchMedia("(hover: hover)")`) — **hover** a pin shows the preview (no button) + highlights the matching card; **mouseout** hides it (130ms debounce so moving between nearby pins isn't janky); **clicking** the pin navigates to the profile. TOUCH — **tap** shows the preview WITH a "Ver perfil"/"View profile" button (localized) and Google's × closes it (no hover auto-close). `closeTimerRef` debounces the close. NOTE: the user's "Buscar en esta área" reference screenshot is NOT our app (no such button/feature exists in code; /buscar searches by provincia/cantón, not map bounds) — treated as style inspiration only. Build green. design-guide §(map) updated.

**Sprint 176 — /buscar MOBILE filters: cramped chips row → tidy "Filtros" bottom-sheet panel.** The mobile filters were a horizontal-scroll row of wide (140–170px) dropdown chips inside the results-sheet header — cramped/cluttered at ~360px. Replaced with the standard mobile pattern: (1) `SearchFilters` gained `hideSearch` + `hideHeader` props; in drawer mode it drops its card chrome/header/search (the sheet supplies them). (2) `buscar/page.tsx` now passes `mobileFilters={<SearchFilters hideSearch hideHeader />}` (the full STACKED sidebar layout, minus the duplicate search). (3) `search-results-layout.tsx`: the sheet header now shows the search bar + a compact **"Filtros (N)"** button (`SlidersHorizontal` + a brand-blue count badge from `activeFilterCount`, computed from the URL params) + the count label; tapping it opens a **bottom-sheet** (`lg:hidden fixed inset-0 z-[60]`, `max-h-[86dvh]`, rounded-top, backdrop) with the filters stacked vertically and a sticky "Ver resultados"/"Show results" footer (filters still apply instantly; footer/×/backdrop just dismiss). New key `search.filters.showResults` (ES/EN). Desktop/lg–xl unchanged; the now-unused `variant="chips"` path remains (harmless). Build green; design-guide §51 updated.

**Sprint 177 — BookingModal ("Ver horario completo" / "Reservar cita") visual redesign to match the target screenshots.** `booking-modal.tsx`, VISUAL only — all logic/steps/validation/data-fetching and the `ProfessionalSchedule` trigger unchanged. (1) **Left navy panel** (`from-[#1a2744] via-[#13294a] to-[#009FD9]`) widened `260px → 320px`, fully CENTERED on desktop (`md:items-center`), and now shows a green **"✓ Verificado"** (`Check` + `#34d399`) under the name for `isVerified` pros (it was missing). "Qué sigue" reworded to the screenshot copy ("Eliges fecha y hora y envías tu solicitud." / "Se abre WhatsApp con el profesional para confirmar." / "Coordinan precio y detalles directamente, sin intermediarios."). (2) **Calendar step is now TWO sub-columns** (`md:grid-cols-2`): the calendar on the left, that day's slots on the right (divider `md:border-l`). Subtitle → "[En {location}.] Los días con punto azul tienen citas." (3) **Calendar cells**: EVERY available day now carries a blue dot (was today-only); today is marked **"HOY"** (8px label, top); selected = filled brand-blue. (4) **Slots grouped by franja** — Mañana (`Sun`) / Tarde (`Sunset`) / Noche (`Moon`), grey icons (serious-app rule), equal-width pills in `grid-cols-3`, with the selected date heading above. When no day is picked, the right column shows an **empty-state** (`CalendarCheck` + "Elige un día disponible para ver las horas."). (5) **Footer (calendar step)**: selected "fecha · hora" on the LEFT (greyed "Selecciona fecha y hora" until picked) + "Continuar" on the RIGHT (disabled until a time is chosen). Kept brand `#009FD9` + Inter (did not switch to the screenshot's `#008ce0`/typeface). Modal `professional` type is the mock `ProfessionalCardData` (has `isVerified`/`hourlyRate`/`professions`, NOT `pricing`/`verificationStatus`) — used those. Build green; design-guide §(booking modal) updated.

**Sprint 178 — /profesionales/[slug] redesign PHASE 1: header card + right contact card (GATED — tabs phase awaits user review).** `profesionales/[slug]/page.tsx`, visual only (logic/i18n/booking trigger unchanged). The old layout was a left `w-72` aside (identity + CTAs) + right tabbed content. New layout matches the screenshots: (1) **full-width HEADER card** on top — circular avatar, business name + the canonical solid-blue **`Badge variant="verified"`** pill (replaces the old green text), personal name, professions (`·`-joined), location line, and a **right-aligned stats strip** (★rating / `reviewCountLabel` · `expYears` años de exp · `portfolioUrls.length` casos de éxito, each a deep-link to its tab; star orange `#ff9b32`, briefcase/camera brand-blue per "match the screenshots"). **Removed the "destacado" ribbon.** (2) **Body reordered to 2 columns**: tab content `lg:order-1` (LEFT), contact card `aside lg:order-2 lg:w-80` (RIGHT, sticky) — mobile keeps the contact card on top. (3) **Contact card slimmed** — identity removed (now in the header); now leads with **"Desde [₡amount blue] /unit"** (`primaryPricingLabel`, split like the /buscar card), then the **filled-blue "Ver horario completo"** as the primary CTA (new `label` prop on `BookingButton`; opens the booking modal), then the existing green WhatsApp, outlined Llamar, optional Correo, and social icons. New `profile` keys: `statYears`/`statCasos`/`from`/`viewFullSchedule` (ES/EN). NOTE: the inline **location-tabs + mini 3-day schedule** from the /buscar card are NOT included yet — this page does not fetch `availability_slots`; "Ver horario completo" → the booking modal carries the full schedule. Flagged to the user for the next phase along with the per-tab redesign. Build green; design-guide §(profile) note pending tab phase.

**Sprint 179 — profile contact card: real location-tabs + 3-day schedule (reuses ProfessionalSchedule, STACKED) + Sobre-mí Verificado pill.** (1) `professional-schedule.tsx` gained a **`stacked`** mode (single vertical column — `locationControl` → `scheduleBody` → buttons, no two-column grid, no `info` slot) + **`showSolicitar`** (in stacked+bookable, also render a secondary outlined **"Solicitar servicio"** below "Ver horario completo"). Defaults false → the /buscar card layout is byte-identical. (2) `profesionales/[slug]/page.tsx` now **fetches `availability_slots`** in `load()` (upcoming, retry-without-optional-cols) into `profileSlots`, and the contact card renders `<ProfessionalSchedule stacked showSolicitar slots={profileSlots} …/>` — so the profile's booking section now MIRRORS the /buscar card: "Desde ₡X /unit" → **location tabs (Estudio San Pedro / A domicilio) that switch the address** → that location's **3-day strip** → **mutually-exclusive buttons** (bookable → Ver horario completo + Solicitar servicio; else Contáctanos por WhatsApp + por llamada) → social icons. This **replaced** the old standalone Ver-horario/WhatsApp/Llamar/Correo buttons (ProfessionalSchedule owns its booking modal + self-action + WhatsApp/Llamar). Removed the now-dead `callDigits`. (3) **Sobre mí → Verificación** now uses the canonical blue `Badge variant="verified"` pill (was green text `#15803d`) — consistent with the header card + the pro's panel. Build green. design-guide §(profile) updated.

**Sprint 180 — unify relative-date ("hace X") formatting app-wide; kill "anteayer".** There were TWO time-ago implementations: `lib/utils.ts` `formatRelativeTime` (ES-only, used by notifications) and a local `timeAgo` in `review-section.tsx` using `Intl.RelativeTimeFormat(..., { numeric: "auto" })` — which renders **"anteayer"** for 2 days (and "antier"), breaking the consistent "hace N días" series. FIX: made **`formatRelativeTime(date, locale="es")` the SINGLE canonical helper** — locale-aware, one scale, correct singular/plural: ES `hace un momento · hace N minutos · hace 1 hora/N horas · ayer · hace N días · hace 1 semana/N semanas · hace 1 mes/N meses · "15 jun 2025"`; EN `just now · N minutes ago · 1 hour/N hours ago · yesterday · N days ago · 1 week/N weeks ago · 1 month/N months ago · "Jun 15, 2025"`. Only **1 day is special-cased to "ayer"/"yesterday"**; 2+ days are always "hace N días" (NO anteayer). Older than ~a year → the ACTUAL date (`toLocaleDateString`), never "hace N años". Deleted `review-section.tsx`'s `timeAgo` (now `formatRelativeTime(createdAt, locale)`); the 3 notification callers (`notification-bell`, `notifications-list`, `notificaciones/page`) now pass `useLocale()` so EN works too. Reviews + notifications are the only relative-time surfaces (requests/projects use absolute dates; admin isn't translated). Build green; design-guide §(dates) added.

**Sprint 181 — profile contact card: bookable buttons = "Ver horario completo" + WhatsApp + Llamar (NO "Solicitar servicio").** Per the user, the profile's stacked schedule should use the WhatsApp + Llamar contact buttons, not "Solicitar servicio". Changed `professional-schedule.tsx` `stacked` mode so the contact buttons ALWAYS render (`{hasSchedule && verHorarioButton}{contactButtons}`): bookable → Ver horario completo (filled) + WhatsApp (green) + Llamar; not bookable → WhatsApp + Llamar (coral note above). **Llamar renders OUTLINED in stacked mode** (`stacked ? border-[#009FD9] text-[#009FD9] : bg-[#009FD9] text-white`) to match the reference; the /buscar card keeps the filled Llamar and its unchanged single-button bookable state. Removed the `showSolicitar` prop + `solicitarButton`. Buttons use the existing `schedule.whatsapp`/`schedule.call` strings ("Contáctanos por WhatsApp"/"Contáctanos por llamada", EN parity intact). Build green; design-guide §(profile) updated.

**Sprint 182 — /buscar map: warm cream/beige low-noise basemap + tighter hover mini-card.** `google-map-panel.tsx`. (1) **Basemap restyle** — `LIGHT_STYLE` reworked from grey Positron to a warm **cream/beige** palette per the reference: land `#f4efe4`, natural `#ece5d6`, near-white roads `#fdfaf3` with warm strokes, highways `#f6efdf`, **water muted blue-grey `#c9d6da`** (not bright default blue). Clutter cut: `labels.icon` off (no business glyphs), `poi` off, `transit` off, road labels off (understated), administrative borders off — but locality/province **text labels kept** (warm-grey `#9b9077`). Applied via inline `styles` on the single map instance (no mapId) → identical on desktop AND mobile. Navy result pins (`#162543`) unchanged → high contrast on the light ground. (2) **Hover mini-card top space** — injected a small CSS override (`.gm-style-iw-c` padding `6px 10px 10px`, `.gm-style-iw-d` padding 0 + overflow hidden, close-button pinned to the corner) so the photo/name sit near the top with no excessive empty band above. Zoom/fitBounds/markers untouched. Build green; design-guide §(map) updated.

**Sprint 183 — soporte WhatsApp line: drop "en horario laboral", warmer wording.** `soporte/page.tsx` rendered `{whatsappPre} {link} · {whatsappPost}` = "¿Prefieres WhatsApp? Escríbenos · en horario laboral". Removed the `· {whatsappPost}` segment (no hours/schedule mention) and the now-unused `whatsappPost` key (ES + EN). Improved the link copy: **`whatsappLink` "Escríbenos" → "Escríbenos y con gusto te ayudamos"** (EN "Message us" → "Message us — we're happy to help"); `whatsappPre` unchanged ("¿Prefieres WhatsApp?" / "Prefer WhatsApp?"). The green WhatsApp link (icon + text → `SUPPORT_WHATSAPP_URL`) stays clickable. Build green; design-guide note added.

**Sprint 142 — remove the hover arrow from the home carousel cards.** The "Profesionales para cada proyecto" category cards (`category-carousel.tsx`) showed an `ArrowRight` icon in the bottom-right that faded in on hover (`opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100`). Removed it entirely (and the now-unused `ArrowRight` import); the card's bottom row is now just the category label. Clean hover behavior kept (subtle `hover:scale-[1.04]` + `hover:z-10`); tap/drag navigation unchanged. Build green.

**Sprint 141 — DURABLE fix for the recurring "location selector disappears" regression.** The multi-location selector on the /buscar card kept appearing/vanishing across edits. **RECURRING ROOT CAUSE:** its data source oscillated — derived from **workplaces** it appeared reliably but filtered loosely; derived from **slots** (Sprint 140, for strict per-location) it filtered correctly but DISAPPEARED whenever a multi-location pro's upcoming slots were only at one location (single slot-group → `length > 1` false → no selector). Each fix for one concern reintroduced the other's bug. **DURABLE FIX (separate the two concerns) in `professional-schedule.tsx`:** (1) selector OPTIONS = the pro's ACTUAL service locations — named `workplaces` UNION extra slot-carried locations (`cov_*` a-domicilio, videoconsulta), keyed by id → appears for EVERY multi-location pro regardless of which locations currently have upcoming slots; (2) schedule FILTER stays STRICT per-location by id (selected location shows only its own slots + location-agnostic "general" slots, never another location's; empty location → honest "no upcoming" state); (3) the control is built ONCE (`locationControl`) and rendered in EVERY branch incl. the no-upcoming state, so selecting an empty location can't hide it / trap the client, and future per-branch layout edits can't drop it. Single-location pros show a plain label, no selector. Keeps `md:pr-9` (clears the favorites bookmark), `appearance-none`+`ChevronDown` (aligned), inside the `relative z-10` action zone (above the clickable-card overlay). **DO NOT revert the selector to a slots-only derivation — that's the regression.** Build green.

**Sprint 140 — /buscar: clickable card (no "Ver perfil completo"), wider map, strict per-location schedule.** (1) **Removed the "Ver perfil completo" link**; the WHOLE card now opens the profile via a stretched low-z overlay `<Link absolute inset-0 z-0 tabIndex={-1} aria-hidden>` in `professional-card.tsx`. Interactive bits sit ABOVE it at `relative z-10` (reviews link, casos/cert links, the action/schedule zone) and keep working; the favorites bookmark (z-20, in `SaveableCard`) is above it too. Keyboard/SR users use the still-focusable name/avatar links (the overlay is aria-hidden). `ArrowRight` import dropped; `card.viewProfile` key now unused (left in messages). (2) **Wider map / narrower cards** (`search-results-layout.tsx`): map column `lg:w-[46%] xl:w-[46%]` (was `40%`/`38%`) → the results list + cards are narrower and the map is bigger on desktop; cards stay clean via single-line truncating summaries; mobile list/map stacking unchanged. (3) **Strict per-location schedule** (`professional-schedule.tsx`): location options are derived from the DISTINCT locations actually present in the slots, and selecting one shows ONLY that location's slots — matched by **location id** (`Set`), NO fallback to "all" (a slot only at B can never appear under A). Dropped the Sprint 136/138 workplace-fallback selector (it could show a non-filtering selector); since the availability editor tags every slot with its `location_id`, genuinely multi-location pros still get the selector and it now filters correctly. Build green.

**Sprint 139 — home carousel tap finally navigates (root cause = pointer capture ate the click).** Sprint 130's drag-guard didn't fix it. **Real cause** (`category-carousel.tsx`): the carousel calls `viewport.setPointerCapture()` on `pointerdown` (for dragging); with capture active, the browser dispatches the follow-up `click` to the CAPTURING element (the viewport), NOT the card's `<a>` — so the `<Link>`'s click/navigation never fired (a tap only operated the carousel). The earlier onClick guard was moot because the anchor's onClick never ran. **Fix:** navigate **programmatically on `pointerup`** when the gesture was a tap (`!moved`): `document.elementFromPoint(clientX, clientY).closest("[data-href]")?.dataset.href` → `router.push(href)` (i18n `useRouter` → locale-correct `/buscar?categoria=<id>`). Independent of where the click lands → reliable on touch + mouse; a real horizontal drag (>10px) just scrolls. Each card's `<Link>` got a `data-href`; the anchor keeps its real `href` (SEO/right-click/keyboard); pointer-generated clicks are cancelled (`e.detail !== 0` → `preventDefault`) to avoid double-nav, while keyboard Enter (`detail === 0`) navigates natively. Build green.

**Sprint 138 — /buscar card: REVERT the summary redesign, then GROW the full card to fit (uniform).** New approach (the "card must not grow" constraint is DROPPED): cards are larger — especially TALLER on mobile — sized for the most-information case so ALL info fits and nothing is cut off. (A) Reverted the two card components to pre-redesign (`2860d4b`); (B) on that fuller base, kept ALL info and let the card grow. **Fixed UNIFORM height `h-[452px]` mobile / `md:h-[244px]` desktop** (every card identical; minimal & info-heavy render the same; extra room reads as breathing space). **Keeps:** company+person, Verificado, professions (primary "+N"), location (primary "+N") + "se desplaza", inline 3-day schedule grid (~2-3 slots), reviews, price, **casos de éxito + certificaciones links**, contact buttons, **"Ver perfil completo"**. **Structure for safety** (`professional-card.tsx`): identity zone is a column = `[avatar+info flex-1 overflow-hidden]` (clips its OWN overflow only in a rare extreme) + `[shrink-0 footer: casos/cert + "Ver perfil completo", ALWAYS visible]`; the action column (schedule+buttons) is content-height and always fully shown (identity is `flex-1` = card−action, so it absorbs/clips, never the actions). So primary actions + "Ver perfil completo" can NEVER be clipped. **Fixes by giving room:** location single-line truncating summaries (never cut off); multi-location selector falls back to the pro's named **workplaces** (appears for multi-workplace pros even when slots aren't tagged) with `md:pr-9` so it never overlaps the favorites bookmark (`professional-schedule.tsx`); selector chevron stays `appearance-none` + `ChevronDown` (aligned); name+company both visible on mobile. Supersedes Sprints 134/135/136 card sizing. Build green. Heights are sized for the realistic max — easy to tune if the live site shows whitespace/clipping.

**Sprint 137 — pro panel: positive "Verifica tu identidad" invitation (no negative label).** In the professional's OWN panel header (`dashboard/profesional/page.tsx`), the negative `proPanel.identityUnverified` ("Identidad sin verificar") warning badge under the name — and the `verificationRejected` error badge — are replaced by a single positive, OPTIONAL **invitation chip "Verifica tu identidad" / "Verify your identity"** (brand-blue `bg-[#EBF5FB] text-[#0089bb]` pill + `ShieldCheck`) shown whenever the pro is NOT verified. Clicking it opens the Verificación tab (`setTab("verificacion")`), where the detailed status + actions (incl. the appeal flow for rejected) live. Verified pros still show the `Badge variant="verified"` "Verificado" and none of the invitation. **Public view unchanged & already label-free** — the /buscar card (`professional-card.tsx`) and public profile (`profesionales/[slug]`) show NO badge/text for unverified pros (verified-only; confirmed). New i18n `proPanel.verifyInvite` (ES/EN); the old `identityUnverified`/`verificationRejected` keys are left (now unused in the header). Build green.

**Sprint 136 — /buscar card: two bugs (Ver perfil always visible on mobile; selector for multi-workplace pros).** (1) **"Ver perfil completo" vanished on info-heavy MOBILE cards.** Cause: it was the last child INSIDE the identity text column, which is `overflow-hidden` + height-limited (`flex-1`); a tall mobile card overflowed and clipped the bottom element. Fix (`professional-card.tsx`): the identity zone is now a COLUMN = an avatar+info block (`flex-1 min-h-0 overflow-hidden`, clips its OWN overflow) + the **"Ver perfil completo" link as a `shrink-0` footer OUTSIDE the clip region**, so it's ALWAYS visible (mobile too); only the least-critical info line can clip in extreme cases. Height unchanged. (2) **Location selector didn't appear for pros with multiple workplaces.** Cause (`professional-schedule.tsx`): selector groups were derived ONLY from `slot.locationId`; a pro with multiple workplaces but generic/untagged slots → single "General" group → no selector. Fix: fall back to the pro's named **workplaces** — trust slot-derived groups when slots carry ≥2 locations, else use the workplaces (≥2). Selecting a place filters to its slots if it has any, otherwise shows ALL bookable times and carries the chosen place into the booking (`initialLocationId`/`Label`) — never empties the grid. Selector keeps `md:pr-9` so it never overlaps the favorites bookmark. Build green.

**Sprint 135 — REVERT Sprint 134's summary card, then arrange the FULL card + remove casos/cert.** The drastic summary-card redesign (Sprint 134) wasn't wanted. Two steps: (A) **Reverted** `professional-card.tsx` + `professional-schedule.tsx` to their pre-redesign state (commit `2860d4b`, before `6b9a13c`) — the familiar card with the inline 3-day availability grid, multi-location selector, reviews, price, contact buttons; the unrelated `availability-editor.tsx` interval-chevron fix was KEPT (it's not the card). (B) On that restored card, made ONE deliberate removal + better ARRANGEMENT (keep everything else): **removed "Casos de éxito" + "Certificaciones" from the card** (they live ONLY on the profile, which already has dedicated tabs for both) and added a clear **"Ver perfil completo →"** lead-in (`card.viewProfile`, reworded to "Ver perfil completo"/"View full profile") in the freed space so clients know to open the profile for full details. **Arrangement fixes:** favorites bookmark no longer overlaps the location selector (schedule's top selector/label row gets `md:pr-9`; the grid is vertically centered, already clear); location is now single truncating lines (primary place "+N" + coverage) so it's never cut off; name + company both stay visible on mobile (kept). KEPT: company+person, Verificado, professions (primary "+N"), schedule (3-day grid ~3 PC), reviews, price, contact buttons. Card height unchanged (`h-[360px] md:h-[232px]`; freed space makes it fit comfortably). ES/EN OK. Build green. **This supersedes Sprint 134 (now reverted).**

**Sprint 134 — /buscar card REDESIGN: a true summary card (fixes overcrowding for good).** ⚠️ **REVERTED in Sprint 135** (the drastic glimpse/removed-selector redesign was undone). After ~10 incremental patches the card still didn't fit everything cleanly. Root cause: it tried to show more than fits uniformly (full inline scheduler + all locations/professions). **Approach: RESTRUCTURE into a scannable SUMMARY** (Airbnb/Thumbtack model) — card = decide-whether-to-open; full detail = on the profile / in the booking modal. **Essential ON the card:** avatar, company (primary) + person (secondary, never dropped on mobile — Sprint 133), Verificado, price, primary profession "+N", rating, PRIMARY location "+N", a soonest-times GLIMPSE (up to 3 quick-book chips "Hoy 15:00"/"Today 15:00") + "Solicitar servicio" (opens the full calendar in `BookingModal`) + WhatsApp. **MOVED to profile/modal:** all addresses, all professions, the full multi-day scheduler + location selector + paging arrows, casos de éxito link, certifications link. **`professional-schedule.tsx` rewritten** (compact): a flat soonest-first `upcoming` list → 3 chips + "+N" (opens booking); removed the day-column grid, the multi-location `<select>`, and the prev/next arrows; private/no-slot branches keep the note + contact buttons. **`professional-card.tsx`:** left column trimmed (casos/cert links removed → profile); location is now ONE primary place "+N" + coverage, each a single truncating line (no wrap-clip / cut-off); imports cleaned (`ImageIcon`/`Award` gone). **Symptoms fixed:** schedule ~2-3 cap (glimpse + modal), bookmark overlapping the location selector (selector gone; glimpse uses `md:pr-9` to clear the top-right bookmark), location/"se desplaza" cut off on PC (single-line summaries + freed space), many addresses/professions don't fit (capped/summarized), "Intervalo/Cada hora" arrow misaligned in `availability-editor.tsx` (native arrow → `appearance-none` + custom aligned `ChevronDown`), personal name vanishing on mobile (kept). **Height FIXED + slightly smaller: `h-[330px] md:h-[216px]`** (sized for the richest case so nothing is ever cut off; all cards uniform whether minimal or maximal info). ES/EN reuse existing `schedule` keys (`upcomingTimes`, `today`, `tomorrow`, `requestService`, `whatsapp`, `viewFullSchedule`). Build green. **This is the card standard — keep it a summary; new detail goes on the profile, not the card.**

**Sprint 133 — /buscar card: personal name never disappears on mobile + tidy mobile price.** Company/brand stays the bold PRIMARY with the person name as the smaller SECONDARY (unchanged, correct; abbreviated first-name + both-surnames form). Mobile bug fixed (the personal name vanished when a card had lots of info) WITHOUT growing the card and WITHOUT touching desktop (`professional-card.tsx`): (1) the person name now has its OWN dedicated secondary line on mobile (`md:hidden` row), so it can never be dropped/clipped by name-line wrapping; (2) the PRICE moves OFF the mobile name line to the right of that secondary line (name line on mobile is just company + Verificado, so it never wraps awkwardly) — right-aligned, `pr-9` clears the bookmark button; (3) DESKTOP unchanged — price far-right on the name line (`hidden md:inline-block md:ml-auto`), person name below (`hidden md:block`). All cases covered: company+person+price, person-only (no company → price right-aligns alone on the mobile secondary line), and no-price. Verificado pill still beside the name; location unchanged; card height still `h-[360px] md:h-[232px]`. No new i18n. Build green.

**Sprint 132 — APP-WIDE SAVE STANDARD: bulletproof reliable autosave (no silent data loss).** Chosen pattern (apply to EVERY editable section, no mix): **RELIABLE AUTOSAVE** — one consistent `SaveStatus` indicator ("Guardando…/Guardado/Sin guardar"), NO save buttons. Made bulletproof so a change made right before leaving a section is never silently lost. **Bug fixed:** editing "nombre comercial" then quickly opening "Profesiones" lost the change — dashboard tabs switch via `router.push` from a `<button>` (not an `<a>`), so the editor UNMOUNTED before the 1.5s debounce fired and `UnsavedChangesGuard` (anchor-only) never triggered; the pending timer was cleared on unmount → data lost. **The standard (3 guarantees):** (1) `touch()` debounces a save (~1s); (2) `flush()` saves immediately and is bound to text-field `onBlur`, so clicking another tab/link — which blurs the focused field first — saves BEFORE navigating; (3) **FLUSH ON UNMOUNT** — a cleanup effect fires the pending save when the editor unmounts (tab switch); the fetch survives the unmount on a same-page tab switch, so it persists. Uses a `saveRef` (latest closure) + `dirtyRef` so the latest values save, and covers non-text changes (toggles, languages) too. `UnsavedChangesGuard` (links + `beforeunload`) stays as the safety net for HARD navigation. **Applied to both debounced editors:** pro `profile-editor.tsx` (Mi perfil) and the client dashboard profile section (`dashboard/cliente/page.tsx`). **Action-based editors** (Servicios `services-editor.tsx`, Disponibilidad `availability-editor.tsx`, Photos `photo-gallery.tsx`) already persist per action (immediate `persist()` on every add/remove/toggle) — inherently safe, unchanged. i18n `saveStatus`/`unsavedGuard` already ES+EN. **Future editable sections MUST follow this standard** (autosave + flush-on-blur + flush-on-unmount + guard, OR per-action immediate persist). Build green.

**Sprint 131 — modernized "Así funciona" phone mockup + faithful /buscar card.** The phone mockup in the "Así funciona ContrataCR" section is `why-contratacr.tsx` → `PhoneFrame` + `ResultsScreen` from **`phone-screens.tsx`** (NOT the dead `phone-mockup.tsx`/`app-section.tsx`, which aren't imported anywhere). **PhoneFrame** redesigned more premium/modern: refined titanium rail, slimmer black bezel, crisper squircle screen (radius 46), machined side buttons, layered depth shadow + a faint top glass sheen. **Inner content** rewritten to faithfully mirror the REAL `/buscar` card (new local `ProCard` replacing `MiniCard`): white `rounded-2xl` + border, `EBF5FB`/`#009FD9` avatar, the SAME solid brand-blue **"Verificado" pill** (`bg-[#009FD9] text-white` — previously an off-brand GREEN text, now correct per `Badge variant="verified"`), price on the right, grey profession chip (`bg-[#f3f4f6]`), orange-star rating (`#ff9b32`) + brand-blue reviews, brand-blue location pin, the inline **availability schedule grid** (3 day columns, `EBF5FB`/`#0089bb` time chips — the hero feature), and the WhatsApp (`#25D366`) + "Solicitar servicio" (`#009FD9`) action row. Featured example is **"SG Solutions"** (company) / Steven Gómez (person secondary), plus Ana Mora. **i18n:** the mockup was hardcoded Spanish; it's now locale-aware — `why-contratacr` builds a `ResultsCopy` reusing the REAL app strings (`card.verifiedShort`, `card.reviewsCount`, `schedule.requestService`/`whatsapp`/`viewFullSchedule`, `categories.plomeria`) + 2 new keys `landing.howItWorks.mockResults`/`mockSearch`, with localized upcoming-date column labels (es-CR/en-US). `ResultsScreen` takes an optional `copy` prop (Spanish defaults kept so the dead `SHOWCASE_SCREENS` ref still renders). Responsive: phone is 284px wide, centered, fits ~360px; section grid stacks (phone on top on mobile). Build green.

**Sprint 130 — home carousel cards weren't navigating to filtered /buscar.** The "Profesionales para cada proyecto" category cards (`category-carousel.tsx`) didn't open `/buscar` filtered by their category. **Root cause was NOT the link or the slug** — the href is `/buscar?categoria=<id>` (i18n `Link`, string+query; same pattern as the shipped `/buscar?verificados=1`), all 19 HOME_CATEGORIES ids are real taxonomy ids, and `/buscar` filters correctly via `category_id.eq.<id> OR professions.cs.{<id>}`. The bug was the **drag-vs-click handling**: after a drag, `endDrag` attached a one-time CAPTURE-phase click swallower with `{ once: true }`, which only self-removes when a click actually fires. A horizontal swipe usually ends on a *different* card, so NO click fires → the swallower **lingers** and then eats the user's **next genuine tap/click**. Constant on mobile (you must swipe to browse, then tap → tap eaten); on desktop it triggered after any drag. **Fix:** replaced the lingering listener with a per-gesture guard — the card's `onClick` cancels navigation ONLY when `drag.current.moved` is true (a real drag), and `moved` resets on every `pointerdown`, so a prior swipe can never block a later clean tap; removed the swallower from `endDrag`; raised the drag threshold 4px → 10px so a finger tap (wobbles a few px) counts as a tap, not a drag. Now every card reliably opens `/buscar` pre-filtered to its category (correct results) on desktop click and mobile tap; ES/EN labels via `getCategoryLabel`. Build green.

**Sprint 129 — footer tagline tighten + "Hecho en Costa Rica" kept.** Footer tagline was long/technical and mentioned free signup. Rewrote concise + appealing, keeping the two essentials (exclusively-CR marketplace + intermediary positioning) and dropping any free promise: ES "El mercado de servicios profesionales exclusivo para Costa Rica. Encuentra al experto ideal y acuerda el pago directamente con él." / EN "The professional services marketplace exclusively for Costa Rica. Find the ideal expert and arrange payment directly with them." **Decision: KEPT "Hecho en Costa Rica"** in the bottom bar — single short grey line (no colorful icon/flag, consistent with the serious tone), reinforces the core exclusively-CR identity; the tagline conveys scope ("para Costa Rica") while "Hecho en Costa Rica" conveys built-here pride (no redundancy). (Email removal from the footer was already done in Sprint 128.) Build green.
